COMMAND:
cd /app && cat > src/core/atomic-selectors.ts <<'EOF'
import {
  BuiltLogic,
  KeaPlugin,
  Logic,
  LogicBuilder,
  LogicPropSelectors,
  Selector,
  SelectorDefinition,
  SelectorDefinitions,
  SelectorHealth,
} from '../types'
import { getContext, getStoreState } from '../kea/context'

/**
 * Atomic Signal Selector Engine
 *
 * When enabled through `resetContext({ atomicSelectors: true })` Kea tracks
 * selector dependencies at the exact leaf level (e.g. `user.name`), supports
 * fine-grained access into Maps, Sets and Arrays, propagates changes only to
 * affected selectors and exposes `logic.selectorHealth()`.
 */

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Internal types
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

const RAW = Symbol('kea.atomic.raw')

type DepSegment =
  | { kind: 'prop'; key: string | number }
  | { kind: 'index'; key: number }
  | { kind: 'mapKey'; key: any }
  | { kind: 'setValue'; value: any }

interface AtomicDependency {
  id: string
  type: 'leaf' | 'selector' | 'props'
  segments?: DepSegment[]
  selectorName?: string
  foreignFn?: Selector
  value: any
}

interface AtomicSelectorMeta {
  name: string
  rootDerived: boolean
  reducerKey?: string
  inputFns?: Selector[]
  computeFn?: (...args: any[]) => any
  memoizeOptions?: Record<string, any>
  value: any
  proxy: any
  dependencies: AtomicDependency[]
  dependents: string[]
  evaluations: number
  dirtyCause: string | null
  dirty: boolean
  hasEvaluated: boolean
}

interface RecomputeSession {
  processed: Set<string>
  visiting: Set<string>
  state: any
  props: any
}

interface AtomicLogicState {
  logic: BuiltLogic
  metas: Record<string, AtomicSelectorMeta>
  lastState: any
  lastProps: any
  mounted: boolean
  proxyCache: WeakMap<object, Map<string, any>>
  recomputeSession: RecomputeSession | null
}

interface InputValue {
  value: any
  baseDep: AtomicDependency | null
}

interface AtomicSelectorFnMeta {
  logic: BuiltLogic
  name: string
  rootDerived: boolean
  props?: boolean
}

type AtomicSelectorFn = Selector & { _keaAtomicMeta?: AtomicSelectorFnMeta }

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Tracker
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

let activeTracker: Tracker | null = null

class Tracker {
  ls: AtomicLogicState
  deps = new Map<string, AtomicDependency>()

  constructor(ls: AtomicLogicState) {
    this.ls = ls
  }

  addDep(dep: AtomicDependency): void {
    if (!this.deps.has(dep.id)) {
      this.deps.set(dep.id, { ...dep, value: unwrapProxy(dep.value) })
    }
  }

  addLeaf(segments: DepSegment[], value: any): void {
    const id = segmentsToId(segments)
    if (!this.deps.has(id)) {
      this.deps.set(id, { id, type: 'leaf', segments, value: unwrapProxy(value) })
    }
  }

  addSelector(name: string, value: any): void {
    const id = `selector:${name}`
    if (!this.deps.has(id)) {
      this.deps.set(id, { id, type: 'selector', selectorName: name, value: unwrapProxy(value) })
    }
  }

  addProps(segments: DepSegment[], value: any): void {
    const id = `props:${segmentsToId(segments)}`
    if (!this.deps.has(id)) {
      this.deps.set(id, { id, type: 'props', segments, value: unwrapProxy(value) })
    }
  }
}

function recordLeaf(ls: AtomicLogicState, segments: DepSegment[], value: any): void {
  const t = activeTracker
  if (t && t.ls === ls) {
    t.addLeaf(segments, value)
  }
}

function recordSelector(ls: AtomicLogicState, name: string, value: any): void {
  const t = activeTracker
  if (t && t.ls === ls && name) {
    t.addSelector(name, value)
  }
}

function recordProps(ls: AtomicLogicState, segments: DepSegment[], value: any): void {
  const t = activeTracker
  if (t && t.ls === ls) {
    t.addProps(segments, value)
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Helpers
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function isTrackableObject(value: any): boolean {
  return value !== null && (typeof value === 'object' || typeof value === 'function')
}

function unwrapProxy(value: any): any {
  if (isTrackableObject(value)) {
    try {
      if (value[RAW] !== undefined) {
        return value[RAW]
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // ignore
    }
  }
  return value
}

function segmentToId(seg: DepSegment): string {
  if (seg.kind === 'prop') return String(seg.key)
  if (seg.kind === 'index') return String(seg.key)
  if (seg.kind === 'mapKey') return `map:${String(seg.key)}`
  return `set:${String(seg.value)}`
}

function segmentsToId(segments: DepSegment[]): string {
  return segments.map(segmentToId).join('.')
}

function isArrayIndex(str: string): number | null {
  if (str === '') return null
  const n = Number(str)
  return Number.isInteger(n) && n >= 0 && String(n) === str ? n : null
}

function getLogicRootRaw(logic: BuiltLogic, state: any): any {
  if (!state) return undefined
  return logic.path.reduce((v, part) => (v == null ? v : v[part]), state)
}

function readValueBySegments(raw: any, segments: DepSegment[]): any {
  let cur = raw
  for (const seg of segments) {
    if (cur == null) return undefined
    if (seg.kind === 'prop') cur = cur[seg.key]
    else if (seg.kind === 'index') cur = cur[seg.key]
    else if (seg.kind === 'mapKey') cur = cur instanceof Map ? cur.get(seg.key) : cur[seg.key]
    else if (seg.kind === 'setValue') cur = cur instanceof Set ? cur.has(seg.value) : false
  }
  return unwrapProxy(cur)
}

function readDependencyValue(dep: AtomicDependency, ls: AtomicLogicState, state: any, props: any): any {
  if (dep.type === 'leaf' && dep.segments) {
    return readValueBySegments(getLogicRootRaw(ls.logic, state), dep.segments)
  }
  if (dep.type === 'props' && dep.segments) {
    return readValueBySegments(props, dep.segments)
  }
  if (dep.type === 'selector') {
    if (dep.foreignFn) {
      return unwrapProxy(dep.foreignFn(state, props))
    }
    const meta = dep.selectorName ? ls.metas[dep.selectorName] : undefined
    return meta ? meta.value : undefined
  }
  return undefined
}

function dependencyEqual(dep: AtomicDependency, current: any, ls: AtomicLogicState): boolean {
  if (dep.type === 'selector' && dep.selectorName) {
    const target = ls.metas[dep.selectorName]
    const eq = target && target.memoizeOptions && target.memoizeOptions.resultEqualityCheck
    if (typeof eq === 'function') {
      return eq(dep.value, current)
    }
  }
  return Object.is(dep.value, current)
}

function sameValue(a: any, b: any, memoizeOptions?: Record<string, any>): boolean {
  const eq = memoizeOptions && memoizeOptions.resultEqualityCheck
  if (typeof eq === 'function') {
    return eq(a, b)
  }
  return Object.is(a, b)
}

function defineValue(logic: BuiltLogic, key: string): void {
  if (!logic.values.hasOwnProperty(key)) {
    Object.defineProperty(logic.values, key, {
      get: function () {
        return logic.selectors[key](getStoreState(), logic.props)
      },
      enumerable: true,
    })
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Proxies
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function cachedProxy(ls: AtomicLogicState, raw: object, pathKey: string, create: () => any): any {
  let m = ls.proxyCache.get(raw)
  if (!m) {
    m = new Map()
    ls.proxyCache.set(raw, m)
  }
  let p = m.get(pathKey)
  if (!p) {
    p = create()
    m.set(pathKey, p)
  }
  return p
}

function createStateProxy(raw: any, base: DepSegment[], ls: AtomicLogicState): any {
  raw = unwrapProxy(raw)
  if (!isTrackableObject(raw)) return raw
  if (Array.isArray(raw)) {
    const pathKey = `a:${segmentsToId(base)}`
    return cachedProxy(ls, raw, pathKey, () => new Proxy(raw, arrayHandler(ls, base)))
  }
  if (raw instanceof Map) {
    const pathKey = `m:${segmentsToId(base)}`
    return cachedProxy(ls, raw, pathKey, () => new Proxy(raw, mapHandler(ls, base)))
  }
  if (raw instanceof Set) {
    const pathKey = `s:${segmentsToId(base)}`
    return cachedProxy(ls, raw, pathKey, () => new Proxy(raw, setHandler(ls, base)))
  }
  const pathKey = `o:${segmentsToId(base)}`
  return cachedProxy(ls, raw, pathKey, () => new Proxy(raw, objectHandler(ls, base)))
}

function wrapReadValue(ls: AtomicLogicState, value: any, segments: DepSegment[]): any {
  return createStateProxy(unwrapProxy(value), segments, ls)
}

const objectHandler = (ls: AtomicLogicState, base: DepSegment[]) => ({
  get(target: any, prop: string | symbol, receiver: any): any {
    if (prop === RAW) return target
    if (typeof prop === 'symbol') {
      return Reflect.get(target, prop, target)
    }
    const segment: DepSegment = { kind: 'prop', key: prop }
    const value = Reflect.get(target, prop, target)
    recordLeaf(ls, [...base, segment], value)
    return wrapReadValue(ls, value, [...base, segment])
  },
  has(target: any, prop: string | symbol): boolean {
    if (typeof prop === 'symbol') return Reflect.has(target, prop)
    const segment: DepSegment = { kind: 'prop', key: prop }
    recordLeaf(ls, [...base, segment], Reflect.get(target, prop))
    return Reflect.has(target, prop)
  },
  ownKeys(target: any): ArrayLike<string | symbol> {
    const keys = Reflect.ownKeys(target)
    for (const key of keys) {
      if (typeof key === 'string') {
        recordLeaf(ls, [...base, { kind: 'prop', key }], Reflect.get(target, key))
      }
    }
    return keys
  },
  getOwnPropertyDescriptor(target: any, prop: string | symbol): PropertyDescriptor | undefined {
    if (typeof prop === 'string') {
      recordLeaf(ls, [...base, { kind: 'prop', key: prop }], Reflect.get(target, prop))
    }
    return Reflect.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(target, prop)
  },
})

function arrayIterator(ls: AtomicLogicState, base: DepSegment[], target: any[], mode: 'values' | 'entries'): Iterator<any> {
  let i = 0
  return {
    next(): IteratorResult<any> {
      if (i < target.length) {
        const idx = i
        const segments = [...base, { kind: 'index', key: idx }]
        recordLeaf(ls, segments, target[idx])
        const value = wrapReadValue(ls, target[idx], segments)
        i++
        if (mode === 'entries') {
          return { value: [idx, value], done: false }
        }
        return { value, done: false }
      }
      return { value: undefined, done: true }
    },
    [Symbol.iterator]() {
      return this
    },
  }
}

function wrapArrayMethod(ls: AtomicLogicState, base: DepSegment[], target: any[], prop: string): (...args: any[]) => any {
  const recordIndex = (i: number) => recordLeaf(ls, [...base, { kind: 'index', key: i }], target[i])
  const proxyArray = createStateProxy(target, base, ls)

  switch (prop) {
    case 'includes':
      return (searchElement: any, fromIndex?: number) => {
        const start = fromIndex === undefined ? 0 : Math.max(fromIndex >= 0 ? fromIndex : target.length + fromIndex, 0)
        for (let i = start; i < target.length; i++) {
          recordIndex(i)
          if (Object.is(target[i], searchElement)) return true
        }
        return false
      }
    case 'indexOf':
      return (searchElement: any, fromIndex?: number) => {
        const start = fromIndex === undefined ? 0 : Math.max(fromIndex >= 0 ? fromIndex : target.length + fromIndex, 0)
        for (let i = start; i < target.length; i++) {
          recordIndex(i)
          if (target[i] === searchElement) return i
        }
        return -1
      }
    case 'lastIndexOf':
      return (searchElement: any, fromIndex?: number) => {
        const start =
          fromIndex === undefined
            ? target.length - 1
            : Math.min(fromIndex >= 0 ? fromIndex : target.length + fromIndex, target.length - 1)
        for (let i = start; i >= 0; i--) {
          recordIndex(i)
          if (target[i] === searchElement) return i
        }
        return -1
      }
    case 'map':
      return (cb: (item: any, index: number, array: any[]) => any, thisArg?: any) => {
        const result: any[] = []
        for (let i = 0; i < target.length; i++) {
          recordIndex(i)
          const segments = [...base, { kind: 'index', key: i }]
          result.push(unwrapProxy(cb.call(thisArg, wrapReadValue(ls, target[i], segments), i, proxyArray)))
        }
        return result
      }
    case 'filter':
      return (cb: (item: any, index: number, array: any[]) => any, thisArg?: any) => {
        const result: any[] = []
        for (let i = 0; i < target.length; i++) {
          recordIndex(i)
          const segments = [...base, { kind: 'index', key: i }]
          if (cb.call(thisArg, wrapReadValue(ls, target[i], segments), i, proxyArray)) {
            result.push(unwrapProxy(target[i]))
          }
        }
        return result
      }
    case 'forEach':
      return (cb: (item: any, index: number, array: any[]) => void, thisArg?: any) => {
        for (let i = 0; i < target.length; i++) {
          recordIndex(i)
          const segments = [...base, { kind: 'index', key: i }]
          cb.call(thisArg, wrapReadValue(ls, target[i], segments), i, proxyArray)
        }
      }
    case 'some':
      return (cb: (item: any, index: number, array: any[]) => any, thisArg?: any) => {
        for (let i = 0; i < target.length; i++) {
          recordIndex(i)
          const segments = [...base, { kind: 'index', key: i }]
          if (cb.call(thisArg, wrapReadValue(ls, target[i], segments), i, proxyArray)) return true
        }
        return false
      }
    case 'every':
      return (cb: (item: any, index: number, array: any[]) => any, thisArg?: any) => {
        for (let i = 0; i < target.length; i++) {
          recordIndex(i)
          const segments = [...base, { kind: 'index', key: i }]
          if (!cb.call(thisArg, wrapReadValue(ls, target[i], segments), i, proxyArray)) return false
        }
        return true
      }
    case 'find':
      return (cb: (item: any, index: number, array: any[]) => any, thisArg?: any) => {
        for (let i = 0; i < target.length; i++) {
          recordIndex(i)
          const segments = [...base, { kind: 'index', key: i }]
          if (cb.call(thisArg, wrapReadValue(ls, target[i], segments), i, proxyArray)) {
            return wrapReadValue(ls, target[i], segments)
          }
        }
        return undefined
      }
    case 'findIndex':
      return (cb: (item: any, index: number, array: any[]) => any, thisArg?: any) => {
        for (let i = 0; i < target.length; i++) {
          recordIndex(i)
          const segments = [...base, { kind: 'index', key: i }]
          if (cb.call(thisArg, wrapReadValue(ls, target[i], segments), i, proxyArray)) return i
        }
        return -1
      }
    case 'reduce':
      return (cb: (acc: any, item: any, index: number, array: any[]) => any, initialValue?: any) => {
        let acc = initialValue
        let start = 0
        if (arguments.length < 2) {
          if (target.length === 0) throw new TypeError('Reduce of empty array with no initial value')
          acc = target[0]
          start = 1
        }
        for (let i = start; i < target.length; i++) {
          recordIndex(i)
          const segments = [...base, { kind: 'index', key: i }]
          acc = cb.call(undefined, acc, wrapReadValue(ls, target[i], segments), i, proxyArray)
        }
        return acc
      }
    case 'reduceRight':
      return (cb: (acc: any, item: any, index: number, array: any[]) => any, initialValue?: any) => {
        let acc = initialValue
        let start = target.length - 1
        if (arguments.length < 2) {
          if (target.length === 0) throw new TypeError('Reduce of empty array with no initial value')
          acc = target[target.length - 1]
          start = target.length - 2
        }
        for (let i = start; i >= 0; i--) {
          recordIndex(i)
          const segments = [...base, { kind: 'index', key: i }]
          acc = cb.call(undefined, acc, wrapReadValue(ls, target[i], segments), i, proxyArray)
        }
        return acc
      }
    case 'at':
      return (index: number) => {
        const idx = index < 0 ? target.length + index : index
        if (idx >= 0 && idx < target.length) {
          recordIndex(idx)
          const segments = [...base, { kind: 'index', key: idx }]
          return wrapReadValue(ls, target[idx], segments)
        }
        return undefined
      }
    case 'values':
      return () => arrayIterator(ls, base, target, 'values')
    case 'entries':
      return () => arrayIterator(ls, base, target, 'entries')
    default:
      return (...args: any[]) => {
        if (prop === 'keys') {
          const result: number[] = []
          for (let i = 0; i < target.length; i++) {
            recordIndex(i)
            result.push(i)
          }
          return result[Symbol.iterator] ? result.values() : result
        }
        for (let i = 0; i < target.length; i++) recordIndex(i)
        return (target as any)[prop](...args)
      }
  }
}

const arrayHandler = (ls: AtomicLogicState, base: DepSegment[]) => {
  const obj = objectHandler(ls, base)
  return {
    ...obj,
    get(target: any, prop: string | symbol, receiver: any): any {
      if (prop === RAW) return target
      if (typeof prop === 'symbol') {
        if (prop === Symbol.iterator) {
          return function () {
            return arrayIterator(ls, base, target, 'values')
          }
        }
        return Reflect.get(target, prop, target)
      }
      const idx = isArrayIndex(String(prop))
      if (idx !== null) {
        const segment: DepSegment = { kind: 'index', key: idx }
        recordLeaf(ls, [...base, segment], target[idx])
        return wrapReadValue(ls, target[idx], [...base, segment])
      }
      if (typeof target[prop] === 'function') {
        return wrapArrayMethod(ls, base, target, String(prop))
      }
      const segment: DepSegment = { kind: 'prop', key: prop }
      recordLeaf(ls, [...base, segment], target[prop])
      return wrapReadValue(ls, target[prop], [...base, segment])
    },
  }
}

function wrapMapIterator(
  ls: AtomicLogicState,
  base: DepSegment[],
  target: Map<any, any>,
  mode: 'keys' | 'values' | 'entries',
): Iterator<any> {
  const entries = Array.from(target.entries())
  let i = 0
  return {
    next(): IteratorResult<any> {
      if (i < entries.length) {
        const [key, value] = entries[i]
        const segment: DepSegment = { kind: 'mapKey', key }
        recordLeaf(ls, [...base, segment], target.get(key))
        const trackedValue = wrapReadValue(ls, value, [...base, segment])
        i++
        if (mode === 'keys') return { value: key, done: false }
        if (mode === 'values') return { value: trackedValue, done: false }
        return { value: [key, trackedValue], done: false }
      }
      return { value: undefined, done: true }
    },
    [Symbol.iterator]() {
      return this
    },
  }
}

const mapHandler = (ls: AtomicLogicState, base: DepSegment[]) => ({
  get(target: Map<any, any>, prop: string | symbol, receiver: any): any {
    if (prop === RAW) return target
    if (typeof prop === 'symbol') {
      if (prop === Symbol.iterator) {
        return function () {
          return wrapMapIterator(ls, base, target, 'entries')
        }
      }
      return Reflect.get(target, prop, target)
    }
    if (prop === 'size') {
      recordLeaf(ls, [...base, { kind: 'prop', key: 'size' }], target.size)
      return target.size
    }
    switch (prop) {
      case 'get':
        return (key: any) => {
          const segment: DepSegment = { kind: 'mapKey', key }
          recordLeaf(ls, [...base, segment], target.get(key))
          return wrapReadValue(ls, target.get(key), [...base, segment])
        }
      case 'has':
        return (key: any) => {
          const segment: DepSegment = { kind: 'mapKey', key }
          recordLeaf(ls, [...base, segment], target.has(key))
          return target.has(key)
        }
      case 'forEach':
        return (cb: (value: any, key: any, map: Map<any, any>) => void, thisArg?: any) => {
          for (const [key, value] of target.entries()) {
            const segment: DepSegment = { kind: 'mapKey', key }
            recordLeaf(ls, [...base, segment], value)
            cb.call(thisArg, wrapReadValue(ls, value, [...base, segment]), key, createStateProxy(target, base, ls))
          }
        }
      case 'keys':
        return () => wrapMapIterator(ls, base, target, 'keys')
      case 'values':
        return () => wrapMapIterator(ls, base, target, 'values')
      case 'entries':
        return () => wrapMapIterator(ls, base, target, 'entries')
      case 'set':
      case 'delete':
      case 'clear':
        return (keyOrValue?: any, value?: any) => {
          if (typeof keyOrValue !== 'undefined') {
            const segment: DepSegment = { kind: 'mapKey', key: keyOrValue }
            recordLeaf(ls, [...base, segment], target.get(keyOrValue))
          }
          return createStateProxy(target, base, ls)
        }
      default:
        return Reflect.get(target, prop, target)
    }
  },
  has(target: Map<any, any>, prop: string | symbol): boolean {
    if (typeof prop === 'symbol') return Reflect.has(target, prop)
    recordLeaf(ls, [...base, { kind: 'mapKey', key: prop }], target.has(prop))
    return Reflect.has(target, prop)
  },
})

function wrapSetIterator(ls: AtomicLogicState, base: DepSegment[], target: Set<any>, mode: 'values' | 'entries'): Iterator<any> {
  const values = Array.from(target.values())
  let i = 0
  return {
    next(): IteratorResult<any> {
      if (i < values.length) {
        const value = values[i]
        const segment: DepSegment = { kind: 'setValue', value }
        recordLeaf(ls, [...base, segment], true)
        const trackedValue = wrapReadValue(ls, value, [...base, segment])
        i++
        if (mode === 'values') return { value: trackedValue, done: false }
        return { value: [trackedValue, trackedValue], done: false }
      }
      return { value: undefined, done: true }
    },
    [Symbol.iterator]() {
      return this
    },
  }
}

const setHandler = (ls: AtomicLogicState, base: DepSegment[]) => ({
  get(target: Set<any>, prop: string | symbol, receiver: any): any {
    if (prop === RAW) return target
    if (typeof prop === 'symbol') {
      if (prop === Symbol.iterator) {
        return function () {
          return wrapSetIterator(ls, base, target, 'values')
        }
      }
      return Reflect.get(target, prop, target)
    }
    if (prop === 'size') {
      recordLeaf(ls, [...base, { kind: 'prop', key: 'size' }], target.size)
      return target.size
    }
    switch (prop) {
      case 'has':
        return (value: any) => {
          const segment: DepSegment = { kind: 'setValue', value }
          recordLeaf(ls, [...base, segment], target.has(value))
          return target.has(value)
        }
      case 'forEach':
        return (cb: (value: any, value2: any, set: Set<any>) => void, thisArg?: any) => {
          for (const value of target.values()) {
            const segment: DepSegment = { kind: 'setValue', value }
            recordLeaf(ls, [...base, segment], true)
            cb.call(thisArg, wrapReadValue(ls, value, [...base, segment]), wrapReadValue(ls, value, [...base, segment]), createStateProxy(target, base, ls))
          }
        }
      case 'keys':
      case 'values':
        return () => wrapSetIterator(ls, base, target, 'values')
      case 'entries':
        return () => wrapSetIterator(ls, base, target, 'entries')
      case 'add':
      case 'delete':
      case 'clear':
        return (value?: any) => {
          if (typeof value !== 'undefined') {
            recordLeaf(ls, [...base, { kind: 'setValue', value }], target.has(value))
          }
          return createStateProxy(target, base, ls)
        }
      default:
        return Reflect.get(target, prop, target)
    }
  },
  has(target: Set<any>, prop: string | symbol): boolean {
    if (typeof prop === 'symbol') return Reflect.has(target, prop)
    recordLeaf(ls, [...base, { kind: 'setValue', value: prop }], target.has(prop))
    return Reflect.has(target, prop)
  },
})

function createDerivedProxy(raw: any, name: string, ls: AtomicLogicState): any {
  raw = unwrapProxy(raw)
  if (!isTrackableObject(raw)) return raw
  return new Proxy(raw, {
    get(target: any, prop: string | symbol, receiver: any): any {
      if (prop === RAW) return target
      recordSelector(ls, name, target)
      return Reflect.get(target, prop, target)
    },
    has(target: any, prop: string | symbol): boolean {
      recordSelector(ls, name, target)
      return Reflect.has(target, prop)
    },
    ownKeys(target: any): ArrayLike<string | symbol> {
      recordSelector(ls, name, target)
      return Reflect.ownKeys(target)
    },
    getOwnPropertyDescriptor(target: any, prop: string | symbol): PropertyDescriptor | undefined {
      recordSelector(ls, name, target)
      return Reflect.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(target, prop)
    },
  })
}

function createForeignProxy(raw: any, name: string, ls: AtomicLogicState): any {
  raw = unwrapProxy(raw)
  if (!isTrackableObject(raw)) return raw
  return new Proxy(raw, {
    get(target: any, prop: string | symbol, receiver: any): any {
      if (prop === RAW) return target
      recordSelector(ls, name, target)
      return Reflect.get(target, prop, target)
    },
    has(target: any, prop: string | symbol): boolean {
      recordSelector(ls, name, target)
      return Reflect.has(target, prop)
    },
    ownKeys(target: any): ArrayLike<string | symbol> {
      recordSelector(ls, name, target)
      return Reflect.ownKeys(target)
    },
    getOwnPropertyDescriptor(target: any, prop: string | symbol): PropertyDescriptor | undefined {
      recordSelector(ls, name, target)
      return Reflect.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(target, prop)
    },
  })
}

function createPropsProxy(raw: any, segments: DepSegment[], ls: AtomicLogicState): any {
  if (!isTrackableObject(raw)) return raw
  return new Proxy(raw, {
    get(target: any, prop: string | symbol, receiver: any): any {
      if (prop === RAW) return target
      if (typeof prop === 'symbol') return Reflect.get(target, prop, target)
      const next: DepSegment[] = [...segments, { kind: 'prop', key: prop }]
      const value = Reflect.get(target, prop, target)
      recordProps(ls, next, value)
      return createPropsProxy(value, next, ls)
    },
    has(target: any, prop: string | symbol): boolean {
      if (typeof prop === 'symbol') return Reflect.has(target, prop)
      recordProps(ls, [...segments, { kind: 'prop', key: prop }], Reflect.get(target, prop))
      return Reflect.has(target, prop)
    },
    ownKeys(target: any): ArrayLike<string | symbol> {
      const keys = Reflect.ownKeys(target)
      for (const key of keys) {
        if (typeof key === 'string') recordProps(ls, [...segments, { kind: 'prop', key }], Reflect.get(target, key))
      }
      return keys
    },
    getOwnPropertyDescriptor(target: any, prop: string | symbol): PropertyDescriptor | undefined {
      if (typeof prop === 'string') recordProps(ls, [...segments, { kind: 'prop', key: prop }], Reflect.get(target, prop))
      return Reflect.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(target, prop)
    },
  })
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Engine state
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

export function isAtomicSelectorsEnabled(): boolean {
  return !!getContext().options.atomicSelectors
}

function ensureAtomicLogicState(logic: BuiltLogic): AtomicLogicState {
  const ctx = getContext()
  let ls = ctx.atomic.logics.get(logic) as AtomicLogicState | undefined
  if (!ls) {
    ls = {
      logic,
      metas: {},
      lastState: undefined,
      lastProps: undefined,
      mounted: false,
      proxyCache: new WeakMap(),
      recomputeSession: null,
    }
    ctx.atomic.logics.set(logic, ls)
  }
  return ls
}

function getAtomicLogicState(logic: BuiltLogic): AtomicLogicState | undefined {
  return getContext().atomic.logics.get(logic) as AtomicLogicState | undefined
}

function registerAtomicSelector(
  ls: AtomicLogicState,
  name: string,
  input: Partial<AtomicSelectorMeta>,
): AtomicSelectorMeta {
  const meta: AtomicSelectorMeta = {
    name,
    rootDerived: false,
    value: undefined,
    proxy: undefined,
    dependencies: [],
    dependents: [],
    evaluations: 0,
    dirtyCause: null,
    dirty: false,
    hasEvaluated: false,
    ...input,
  }
  ls.metas[name] = meta
  return meta
}

function makeAtomicSelectorFn(ls: AtomicLogicState, name: string, rootDerived: boolean, getter: Selector): AtomicSelectorFn {
  const fn = ((state: any = getStoreState(), props: any = ls.logic.props) => getter(state, props)) as AtomicSelectorFn
  fn._keaAtomicMeta = { logic: ls.logic, name, rootDerived }
  return fn
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Evaluation
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function evaluateAtomicSelector(ls: AtomicLogicState, name: string, state: any, props: any): any {
  if (state === undefined) state = getStoreState()
  if (props === undefined) props = ls.logic.props
  if (state !== ls.lastState || props !== ls.lastProps) {
    invalidateLogic(ls, state, props)
  }
  const meta = ls.metas[name]
  if (!meta) {
    throw new Error(`[KEA] Atomic selector "${name}" not found in logic "${ls.logic.pathString}"`)
  }
  if (meta.hasEvaluated && !meta.dirty) {
    return meta.proxy
  }
  recompute(ls, state, props)
  return ls.metas[name].proxy
}

function invalidateLogic(ls: AtomicLogicState, state: any, props: any): void {
  if (state === ls.lastState && props === ls.lastProps) return
  ls.lastState = state
  ls.lastProps = props
  for (const meta of Object.values(ls.metas)) {
    if (!meta.hasEvaluated) continue
    for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
      const current = readDependencyValue(dep, ls, state, props)
      if (!dependencyEqual(dep, current, ls)) {
        meta.dirty = true
        if (meta.dirtyCause === null) {
          meta.dirtyCause = dep.id
        }
        break
      }
    }
  }
}

function createSession(ls: AtomicLogicState, state: any, props: any): RecomputeSession {
  for (const meta of Object.values(ls.metas)) {
    if (!meta.hasEvaluated) meta.dirty = true
  }
  const session: RecomputeSession = { processed: new Set(), visiting: new Set(), state, props }
  ls.recomputeSession = session
  return session
}

function recompute(ls: AtomicLogicState, state: any, props: any): void {
  const existing = ls.recomputeSession
  const nested = existing !== null && existing.state === state
  const session = nested ? existing : createSession(ls, state, props)

  const order = buildTopologicalOrder(ls)
  for (const name of order) {
    evaluateDirty(ls, name, session)
  }

  if (!nested) {
    ls.recomputeSession = null
    for (const meta of Object.values(ls.metas)) {
      if (meta.dirty) {
        throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
      }
    }
  }
}

function evaluateDirty(ls: AtomicLogicState, name: string, session: RecomputeSession): void {
  const meta = ls.metas[name]
  if (!meta) return
  if (!meta.dirty || session.processed.has(name)) return
  if (session.visiting.has(name)) {
    throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
  }
  session.visiting.add(name)
  try {
    // evaluate local selector dependencies first
    for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
      if (dep.type === 'selector' && !dep.foreignFn && dep.selectorName && ls.metas[dep.selectorName]?.dirty) {
        evaluateDirty(ls, dep.selectorName, session)
      }
    }
    if (session.processed.has(name)) return
    if (!meta.dirty) return
    evaluateOne(ls, name, session.state, session.props)
    session.processed.add(name)
  } finally {
    session.visiting.delete(name)
  }
}

function getLocalAtomicValue(ls: AtomicLogicState, name: string, state: any, props: any): any {
  const meta = ls.metas[name]
  if (meta.hasEvaluated && !meta.dirty) return meta.proxy
  const existing = ls.recomputeSession
  const session = existing && existing.state === state ? existing : createSession(ls, state, props)
  evaluateDirty(ls, name, session)
  if (existing === null || existing.state !== state) {
    ls.recomputeSession = null
  }
  return ls.metas[name].proxy
}

function evaluateOne(ls: AtomicLogicState, name: string, state: any, props: any): void {
  const meta = ls.metas[name]
  const oldValue = meta.value
  const hadEvaluated = meta.hasEvaluated

  const tracker = new Tracker(ls)
  const previousTracker = activeTracker
  activeTracker = tracker
  let rawResult: any
  try {
    if (meta.rootDerived) {
      const rootRaw = getLogicRootRaw(ls.logic, state)
      const key = meta.reducerKey || name
      const raw = rootRaw == null ? undefined : rootRaw[key]
      tracker.addLeaf([{ kind: 'prop', key }], raw)
      rawResult = raw
    } else {
      rawResult = evaluateCompute(ls, meta, state, props, tracker)
    }
  } finally {
    activeTracker = previousTracker
  }

  const newRaw = unwrapProxy(rawResult)
  meta.value = newRaw
  meta.proxy = meta.rootDerived
    ? createStateProxy(newRaw, meta.reducerKey ? [{ kind: 'prop', key: meta.reducerKey }] : [], ls)
    : createDerivedProxy(newRaw, name, ls)
  meta.dependencies = Array.from(tracker.deps.values())
  meta.evaluations += 1
  meta.hasEvaluated = true
  meta.dirty = false

  rebuildDependents(ls)

  if (!hadEvaluated || !sameValue(oldValue, newRaw, meta.memoizeOptions)) {
    for (const depName of meta.dependents) {
      const depMeta = ls.metas[depName]
      if (depMeta && !depMeta.dirty) {
        const hasSelectorDep = depMeta.dependencies.some(
          (d) => d.type === 'selector' && d.selectorName === name,
        )
        if (hasSelectorDep) {
          depMeta.dirty = true
          depMeta.dirtyCause = `selector:${name}`
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

function evaluateCompute(
  ls: AtomicLogicState,
  meta: AtomicSelectorMeta,
  state: any,
  props: any,
  tracker: Tracker,
): any {
  const inputFns = meta.inputFns || []
  const inputValues: any[] = []
  const inputBases: (AtomicDependency | null)[] = []

  for (const fn of inputFns) {
    const input = getInputValue(ls, fn, state, props)
    inputValues.push(input.value)
    inputBases.push(input.baseDep)
    if (input.baseDep && !isTrackableObject(unwrapProxy(input.value))) {
      tracker.addDep(input.baseDep)
    }
  }

  const result = meta.computeFn ? meta.computeFn(...inputValues) : undefined

  // If the result is exactly one of the input values (identity selector),
  // record the base dependency so the whole object is tracked.
  for (let i = 0; i < inputValues.length; i++) {
    if (inputBases[i]) {
      const unwrappedInput = unwrapProxy(inputValues[i])
      if (result === inputValues[i] || (isTrackableObject(result) && unwrapProxy(result) === unwrappedInput)) {
        tracker.addDep(inputBases[i] as AtomicDependency)
      }
    }
  }

  return result
}

function getInputValue(ls: AtomicLogicState, fn: AtomicSelectorFn, state: any, props: any): InputValue {
  const m = fn._keaAtomicMeta

  if (m && m.props) {
    const raw = props == null ? undefined : props[m.name]
    const segments: DepSegment[] = [{ kind: 'prop', key: m.name }]
    const value = createPropsProxy(raw, segments, ls)
    return { value, baseDep: { id: `props:${segmentsToId(segments)}`, type: 'props', segments, value: unwrapProxy(value) } }
  }

  if (m && m.logic === ls.logic) {
    const value = getLocalAtomicValue(ls, m.name, state, props)
    if (m.rootDerived) {
      const segments: DepSegment[] = [{ kind: 'prop', key: m.name }]
      return { value, baseDep: { id: segmentsToId(segments), type: 'leaf', segments, value: unwrapProxy(value) } }
    }
    return { value, baseDep: { id: `selector:${m.name}`, type: 'selector', selectorName: m.name, value: unwrapProxy(value) } }
  }

  // foreign / plain selector
  const rawValue = unwrapProxy(fn(state, props))
  const localName = findLocalSelectorName(ls, fn)
  const name = localName || 'external'
  const value = createForeignProxy(rawValue, name, ls)
  const dep: AtomicDependency = {
    id: `selector:${name}`,
    type: 'selector',
    selectorName: name,
    foreignFn: fn,
    value: rawValue,
  }
  return { value, baseDep: dep }
}

function findLocalSelectorName(ls: AtomicLogicState, fn: Selector): string | null {
  for (const [name, sel] of Object.entries(ls.logic.selectors)) {
    if (sel === fn) return name
  }
  return null
}

function rebuildDependents(ls: AtomicLogicState): void {
  for (const meta of Object.values(ls.metas)) {
    meta.dependents = []
  }
  for (const [name, meta] of Object.entries(ls.metas)) {
    for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
      if (dep.type === 'selector' && dep.selectorName && ls.metas[dep.selectorName]) {
        ls.metas[dep.selectorName].dependents.push(name)
      } else if (dep.type === 'leaf' && dep.segments && dep.segments.length > 0) {
        const root = String(dep.segments[0].key)
        if (ls.metas[root]) {
          ls.metas[root].dependents.push(name)
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

function buildTopologicalOrder(ls: AtomicLogicState): string[] {
  const result: string[] = []
  const visited = new Set<string>()
  const visiting = new Set<string>()

  const visit = (name: string) => {
    if (visited.has(name)) return
    if (visiting.has(name)) {
      throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
    }
    visiting.add(name)
    const meta = ls.metas[name]
    if (meta) {
      for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
        if (dep.type === 'selector' && !dep.foreignFn && dep.selectorName && ls.metas[dep.selectorName]) {
          visit(dep.selectorName)
        }
      }
    }
    visiting.delete(name)
    visited.add(name)
    result.push(name)
  }

  for (const name of Object.keys(ls.metas)) {
    visit(name)
  }
  return result
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Builders
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

export function atomicSelectors<L extends Logic = Logic>(
  input: SelectorDefinitions<L> | ((logic: L) => SelectorDefinitions<L>),
): LogicBuilder<L> {
  return (logic) => {
    const selectorInputs = typeof input === 'function' ? input(logic) : input
    const ls = ensureAtomicLogicState(logic)

    // create placeholder selector functions so cross-references resolve regardless of order
    for (const key of Object.keys(selectorInputs)) {
      if (typeof logic.selectors[key] !== 'undefined') {
        throw new Error(`[KEA] Logic "${logic.pathString}" selector "${key}" already exists`)
      }
      const placeholder = makeAtomicSelectorFn(ls, key, false, (state, props) => logic.selectors[key](state, props))
      logic.selectors[key] = placeholder
      defineValue(logic, key)
    }

    const propSelectors = createAtomicPropSelectors(logic, ls)

    for (const entry of Object.entries(selectorInputs)) {
      const [key, arr]: [string, SelectorDefinition<any, any, any> | undefined] = entry
      if (!arr) {
        throw new Error(`[KEA] Logic "${logic.pathString}" selector "${key}" is undefined`)
      }
      const [input, func, memoizeOptions] = arr
      const inputFns = input(logic.selectors, propSelectors) as Selector[]

      if (inputFns.filter((a) => typeof a !== 'function').length > 0) {
        const argTypes = inputFns.map((a) => typeof a).join(', ')
        const msg = `[KEA] Logic "${logic.pathString}", selector "${key}" has incorrect input: [${argTypes}].`
        throw new Error(msg)
      }

      registerAtomicSelector(ls, key, {
        rootDerived: false,
        inputFns,
        computeFn: func,
        memoizeOptions,
      })

      const realFn = makeAtomicSelectorFn(ls, key, false, (state, props) => evaluateAtomicSelector(ls, key, state, props))
      logic.selectors[key] = realFn
      defineValue(logic, key)
    }
  }
}

export function addAtomicReducerSelector(logic: BuiltLogic, key: string): Selector {
  const ls = ensureAtomicLogicState(logic)
  if (!logic.selector) {
    logic.selector = createAtomicRootSelector(logic)
  }
  registerAtomicSelector(ls, key, { rootDerived: true, reducerKey: key })
  const fn = makeAtomicSelectorFn(ls, key, true, (state, props) => evaluateAtomicSelector(ls, key, state, props))
  logic.selectors[key] = fn
  defineValue(logic, key)
  return fn
}

export function createAtomicRootSelector(logic: BuiltLogic): Selector {
  const ls = ensureAtomicLogicState(logic)
  const fn = ((state: any = getStoreState(), props: any = logic.props) =>
    createStateProxy(getLogicRootRaw(logic, state), [], ls)) as AtomicSelectorFn
  fn._keaAtomicMeta = { logic, name: '*', rootDerived: true }
  return fn
}

function createAtomicPropSelectors<L extends Logic = Logic>(logic: BuiltLogic, ls: AtomicLogicState): LogicPropSelectors<L> {
  const makePropSelector = (key: string): Selector => {
    const fn = ((state: any, props: any = logic.props) => {
      const segments: DepSegment[] = [{ kind: 'prop', key }]
      const raw = props == null ? undefined : props[key]
      return createPropsProxy(raw, segments, ls)
    }) as AtomicSelectorFn
    fn._keaAtomicMeta = { logic, name: key, rootDerived: false, props: true }
    return fn
  }

  if (typeof Proxy !== 'undefined') {
    return new Proxy(logic.props, {
      get(target, prop) {
        if (!(prop in target)) {
          throw new Error(
            `[KEA] Prop "${String(prop)}" not found for logic "${logic.pathString}". Attempted to use in a selector. Please specify a default via props({ ${String(
              prop,
            )}: '' }) to resolve.`,
          )
        }
        return makePropSelector(String(prop))
      },
    }) as LogicPropSelectors<L>
  }

  return Object.fromEntries(
    Object.keys(logic.props).map((key) => [key, makePropSelector(key)]),
  ) as LogicPropSelectors<L>
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Health API
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function getHealth(logic: BuiltLogic): SelectorHealth {
  const ls = getAtomicLogicState(logic)
  if (!ls) {
    return { selectors: {}, topologicalOrder: [] }
  }
  const selectors: Record<string, any> = {}
  for (const [name, meta] of Object.entries(ls.metas)) {
    selectors[name] = {
      dependencies: meta.dependencies.map((d) => (d.type === 'selector' ? d.selectorName || 'external' : d.id)),
      dependents: [...meta.dependents],
      evaluations: meta.evaluations,
      dirtyCause: meta.dirtyCause,
    }
  }
  return { selectors, topologicalOrder: buildTopologicalOrder(ls) }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Build-time cycle detection
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function detectCycles(ls: AtomicLogicState): void {
  const visited = new Set<string>()
  const visiting = new Set<string>()

  const visit = (name: string) => {
    if (visited.has(name)) return
    if (visiting.has(name)) {
      throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
    }
    visiting.add(name)
    const meta = ls.metas[name]
    if (meta && meta.inputFns) {
      for (const fn of meta.inputFns) {
        const m = (fn as AtomicSelectorFn)._keaAtomicMeta
        if (m && m.logic === ls.logic && m.name && !m.props) {
          visit(m.name)
        }
      }
    }
    visiting.delete(name)
    visited.add(name)
  }

  for (const name of Object.keys(ls.metas)) {
    visit(name)
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Subscription
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function ensureSubscription(): void {
  const ctx = getContext()
  if (ctx.atomic.subscribed) return
  const store = ctx.store
  if (!store) return
  ctx.atomic.subscribed = true
  ctx.atomic.unsubscribe = store.subscribe(() => {
    invalidateAllMountedLogics()
  })
}

function invalidateAllMountedLogics(): void {
  const ctx = getContext()
  const store = ctx.store
  if (!store) return
  const state = store.getState()
  for (const ls of ctx.atomic.logics.values()) {
    if (ls.mounted) {
      invalidateLogic(ls, state, ls.logic.props)
    }
  }
}

function finalizeAtomicLogic(logic: BuiltLogic): void {
  const ls = getAtomicLogicState(logic)
  if (!ls) return
  logic.selectorHealth = () => getHealth(logic)
  detectCycles(ls)
  ensureSubscription()
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Plugin
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const atomicSelectorsPlugin: KeaPlugin = {
  name: 'atomicSelectors',

  defaults: () => ({
    selectorHealth: undefined,
  }),

  events: {
    afterBuild(logic: BuiltLogic): void {
      if (getContext().options.atomicSelectors) {
        finalizeAtomicLogic(logic)
      }
    },
    afterMount(logic: BuiltLogic): void {
      const ls = getAtomicLogicState(logic)
      if (ls) ls.mounted = true
    },
    afterUnmount(logic: BuiltLogic): void {
      const ls = getAtomicLogicState(logic)
      if (ls) ls.mounted = false
    },
    beforeCloseContext(): void {
      const ctx = getContext()
      if (ctx.atomic.unsubscribe) {
        ctx.atomic.unsubscribe()
        ctx.atomic.unsubscribe = null
      }
      ctx.atomic.subscribed = false
    },
  },
}

STEP 45 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cd /app && cat > test/jest/atomic-selectors.js <<'EOF'
import { kea, resetContext, actions, reducers, selectors } from '../../src'

describe('atomic selectors', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    resetContext({ atomicSelectors: true, createStore: true })
  })

  test('tracks leaf dependencies and skips unrelated changes', () => {
    let userNameRuns = 0
    const logic = kea([
      actions({
        setUserName: (name) => ({ name }),
        setUserAge: (age) => ({ age }),
      }),
      reducers({
        user: [
          { name: 'chirpy', age: 30 },
          {
            setUserName: (state, { name }) => ({ ...state, name }),
            setUserAge: (state, { age }) => ({ ...state, age }),
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        userName: [
          (s) => [s.user],
          (user) => {
            userNameRuns += 1
            return user.name
          },
        ],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()

    expect(logic.values.userName).toBe('chirpy')
    expect(userNameRuns).toBe(1)

    logic.actions.setUserAge(31)

    expect(logic.values.userName).toBe('chirpy')
    expect(userNameRuns).toBe(1)

    logic.actions.setUserName('pollo')

    expect(logic.values.userName).toBe('pollo')
    expect(userNameRuns).toBe(2)

    unmount()
  })

  test('selectorHealth exposes dependencies, dependents and dirtyCause', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({
        setUserName: (name) => ({ name }),
        setUserAge: (age) => ({ age }),
      }),
      reducers({
        user: [
          { name: 'chirpy', age: 30 },
          {
            setUserName: (state, { name }) => ({ ...state, name }),
            setUserAge: (state, { age }) => ({ ...state, age }),
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        userName: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.name],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    logic.values.userName

    const health = logic.selectorHealth()
    expect(health.selectors.userName.dependencies).toContain('user.name')
    expect(health.selectors.user.dependents).toContain('userName')
    expect(health.topologicalOrder).toEqual(['user', 'userName'])

    logic.actions.setUserAge(31)
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.userName.dirtyCause).toBe('user.name')
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.userName.evaluations).toBe(1)

    logic.actions.setUserName('pollo')
    logic.values.userName
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.userName.evaluations).toBe(2)

    unmount()
  })

  test('selectorHealth is undefined when disabled', () => {
    resetContext({ createStore: true })
    const logic = kea({
      reducers: { value: ['x', {}] },
    })
    logic.mount()
    expect(logic.selectorHealth).toBeUndefined()
    logic.unmount()
  })
})
EOF

import { kea, resetContext, actions, reducers, selectors } from '../../src'

describe('atomic selectors', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    resetContext({ atomicSelectors: true, createStore: true })
  })

  test('tracks leaf dependencies and skips unrelated changes', () => {
    let userNameRuns = 0
    const logic = kea([
      actions({
        setUserName: (name) => ({ name }),
        setUserAge: (age) => ({ age }),
      }),
      reducers({
        user: [
          { name: 'chirpy', age: 30 },
          {
            setUserName: (state, { name }) => ({ ...state, name }),
            setUserAge: (state, { age }) => ({ ...state, age }),
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        userName: [
          (s) => [s.user],
          (user) => {
            userNameRuns += 1
            return user.name
          },
        ],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()

    expect(logic.values.userName).toBe('chirpy')
    expect(userNameRuns).toBe(1)

    logic.actions.setUserAge(31)

    expect(logic.values.userName).toBe('chirpy')
    expect(userNameRuns).toBe(1)

    logic.actions.setUserName('pollo')

    expect(logic.values.userName).toBe('pollo')
    expect(userNameRuns).toBe(2)

    unmount()
  })

  test('selectorHealth exposes dependencies, dependents and dirtyCause', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({
        setUserName: (name) => ({ name }),
        setUserAge: (age) => ({ age }),
      }),
      reducers({
        user: [
          { name: 'chirpy', age: 30 },
          {
            setUserName: (state, { name }) => ({ ...state, name }),
            setUserAge: (state, { age }) => ({ ...state, age }),
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        userName: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.name],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    logic.values.userName

    const health = logic.selectorHealth()
    expect(health.selectors.userName.dependencies).toContain('user.name')
    expect(health.selectors.user.dependents).toContain('userName')
    expect(health.topologicalOrder).toEqual(['user', 'userName'])

    // unrelated change should not invalidate userName
    logic.actions.setUserAge(31)
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.userName.dirtyCause).toBeNull()
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.user.dirtyCause).toBe('user')

    // related change invalidates userName
    logic.actions.setUserName('pollo')
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.userName.dirtyCause).toBe('user.name')

    logic.values.userName
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.userName.evaluations).toBe(2)

    unmount()
  })

  test('selectorHealth is undefined when disabled', () => {
    resetContext({ createStore: true })
    const logic = kea({
      reducers: { value: ['x', {}] },
    })
    logic.mount()
    expect(logic.selectorHealth).toBeUndefined()
    logic.unmount()
  })
})

cd /app && cat > test/jest/atomic-advanced.js <<'EOF'
import { kea, resetContext, actions, reducers, selectors } from '../../src'

describe('atomic selectors advanced', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    resetContext({ atomicSelectors: true, createStore: true })
  })

  test('multi-level chains propagate only to affected selectors', () => {
    const runs = { level1: 0, level2: 0, level3: 0 }
    const logic = kea([
      actions({
        setA: (a) => ({ a }),
        setB: (b) => ({ b }),
      }),
      reducers({
        a: ['a1', { setA: (_, { a }) => a }],
        b: ['b1', { setB: (_, { b }) => b }],
      }),
      selectors({
        level1: [
          (s) => [s.a, s.b],
          (a, b) => {
            runs.level1 += 1
            return { a, b }
          },
        ],
        level2: [
          (s) => [s.level1],
          (level1) => {
            runs.level2 += 1
            return level1.a
          },
        ],
        level3: [
          (s) => [s.level2],
          (level2) => {
            runs.level3 += 1
            return level2.toUpperCase()
          },
        ],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.level3).toBe('A1')
    expect(runs).toEqual({ level1: 1, level2: 1, level3: 1 })

    // changing b should not re-evaluate level2/level3
    logic.actions.setB('b2')
    expect(logic.values.level3).toBe('A1')
    expect(runs).toEqual({ level1: 2, level2: 1, level3: 1 })

    // changing a propagates through all levels
    logic.actions.setA('a2')
    expect(logic.values.level3).toBe('A2')
    expect(runs).toEqual({ level1: 3, level2: 2, level3: 2 })

    unmount()
  })

  test('atomic updates: one re-evaluation per action even with multiple dependency changes', () => {
    const runs = { sum: 0, product: 0 }
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setBoth: (x, y) => ({ x, y }) }),
      reducers({
        x: [2, { setBoth: (_, { x }) => x }],
        y: [3, { setBoth: (_, { y }) => y }],
      }),
      selectors({
        sum: [
          (s) => [s.x, s.y],
          (x, y) => {
            runs.sum += 1
            return x + y
          },
        ],
        product: [
          (s) => [s.x, s.y],
          (x, y) => {
            runs.product += 1
            return x * y
          },
        ],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.sum).toBe(5)
    expect(logic.values.product).toBe(6)

    logic.actions.setBoth(4, 5)
    expect(logic.values.sum).toBe(9)
    expect(logic.values.product).toBe(20)
    expect(runs.sum).toBe(2)
    expect(runs.product).toBe(2)

    unmount()
  })

  test('dirtyCause uses selector:name when caused by another selector', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setA: (a) => ({ a }) }),
      reducers({ a: ['x', { setA: (_, { a }) => a }] }),
      selectors({
        upper: [(s) => [s.a], (a) => a.toUpperCase()],
        doubled: [(s) => [s.upper], (upper) => upper + upper],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    logic.values.doubled

    logic.actions.setA('y')
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.upper.dirtyCause).toBe('a')
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.doubled.dirtyCause).toBe('selector:upper')

    unmount()
  })

  test('Map key access uses map:key dependency', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setMap: (map) => ({ map }) }),
      reducers({
        data: [new Map([['a', 1], ['b', 2]]), { setMap: (_, { map }) => map }],
      }),
      selectors({
        getA: [(s) => [s.data], (data) => data.get('a')],
        hasB: [(s) => [s.data], (data) => data.has('b')],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.getA).toBe(1)
    expect(logic.values.hasB).toBe(true)

    const health = logic.selectorHealth()
    expect(health.selectors.getA.dependencies).toContain('data.map:a')
    expect(health.selectors.hasB.dependencies).toContain('data.map:b')

    // change only b
    logic.actions.setMap(new Map([['a', 1], ['b', 99]]))
    expect(logic.values.getA).toBe(1)
    expect(logic.values.hasB).toBe(true)
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.getA.evaluations).toBe(1)

    // change a
    logic.actions.setMap(new Map([['a', 10], ['b', 99]]))
    expect(logic.values.getA).toBe(10)
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.getA.evaluations).toBe(2)

    unmount()
  })

  test('Set membership uses set:value dependency', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setData: (set) => ({ set }) }),
      reducers({
        data: [new Set(['a', 'b']), { setData: (_, { set }) => set }],
      }),
      selectors({
        hasA: [(s) => [s.data], (data) => data.has('a')],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.hasA).toBe(true)

    const health = logic.selectorHealth()
    expect(health.selectors.hasA.dependencies).toContain('data.set:a')

    logic.actions.setData(new Set(['b', 'c']))
    expect(logic.values.hasA).toBe(false)
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.hasA.evaluations).toBe(2)

    unmount()
  })

  test('Array includes tracks indices checked', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setList: (list) => ({ list }) }),
      reducers({
        list: [['a', 'b', 'c'], { setList: (_, { list }) => list }],
      }),
      selectors({
        includesB: [(s) => [s.list], (list) => list.includes('b')],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.includesB).toBe(true)

    const health = logic.selectorHealth()
    expect(health.selectors.includesB.dependencies).toContain('list.0')
    expect(health.selectors.includesB.dependencies).toContain('list.1')

    // change an element after the matched one -> no re-eval
    logic.actions.setList(['a', 'b', 'Z'])
    expect(logic.values.includesB).toBe(true)
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.includesB.evaluations).toBe(1)

    // change the matched element -> re-eval
    logic.actions.setList(['a', 'X', 'Z'])
    expect(logic.values.includesB).toBe(false)
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.includesB.evaluations).toBe(2)

    unmount()
  })

  test('circular dependencies throw during build', () => {
    expect(() => {
      kea([
        selectors({
          a: [(s) => [s.b], (b) => b + 1],
          b: [(s) => [s.a], (a) => a + 1],
        }),
      ])
    }).toThrow('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
  })

  test('works with multi-level chain where derived selector caches intermediate', () => {
    const runs = { info: 0, name: 0 }
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setAge: (age) => ({ age }), setName: (name) => ({ name }) }),
      reducers({
        user: [
          { name: 'x', age: 1 },
          {
            setAge: (state, { age }) => ({ ...state, age }),
            setName: (state, { name }) => ({ ...state, name }),
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        userInfo: [
          (s) => [s.user],
          (user) => {
            runs.info += 1
            return { name: user.name }
          },
        ],
        userName: [
          (s) => [s.userInfo],
          (userInfo) => {
            runs.name += 1
            return userInfo.name
          },
        ],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.userName).toBe('x')
    expect(runs).toEqual({ info: 1, name: 1 })

    logic.actions.setAge(2)
    expect(logic.values.userName).toBe('x')
    expect(runs).toEqual({ info: 1, name: 1 })

    logic.actions.setName('y')
    expect(logic.values.userName).toBe('y')
    expect(runs).toEqual({ info: 2, name: 2 })

    unmount()
  })
})
EOF

})

p='src/core/atomic-selectors.ts'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace("""function evaluateAtomicSelector(ls: AtomicLogicState, name: string, state: any, props: any): any {
  if (state === undefined) state = getStoreState()
  if (props === undefined) props = ls.logic.props
  if (state !== ls.lastState || props !== ls.lastProps) {
    invalidateLogic(ls, state, props)
  }""", """function evaluateAtomicSelector(ls: AtomicLogicState, name: string, state: any, props: any): any {
  if (state === undefined) state = getStoreState()
  if (props === undefined) props = ls.logic.props
  if (typeof (global as any).__atomicDebug !== 'undefined') {
    ;(global as any).__atomicDebug(`evaluateAtomicSelector ${name} stateSame=${state === ls.lastState} dirty=${ls.metas[name] && ls.metas[name].dirty}`)
  }
  if (state !== ls.lastState || props !== ls.lastProps) {
    invalidateLogic(ls, state, props)
  }""")
s=s.replace("""function invalidateLogic(ls: AtomicLogicState, state: any, props: any): void {
  if (state === ls.lastState && props === ls.lastProps) return
  ls.lastState = state
  ls.lastProps = props
  for (const meta of Object.values(ls.metas)) {
    if (!meta.hasEvaluated) continue
    for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
      const current = readDependencyValue(dep, ls, state, props)
      if (!dependencyEqual(dep, current, ls)) {
        meta.dirty = true
        if (meta.dirtyCause === null) {
          meta.dirtyCause = dep.id
        }
        break
      }
    }
  }
}""", """function invalidateLogic(ls: AtomicLogicState, state: any, props: any): void {
  if (typeof (global as any).__atomicDebug !== 'undefined') {
    ;(global as any).__atomicDebug(`invalidateLogic stateSame=${state === ls.lastState} propsSame=${props === ls.lastProps}`)
  }
  if (state === ls.lastState && props === ls.lastProps) return
  ls.lastState = state
  ls.lastProps = props
  for (const meta of Object.values(ls.metas)) {
    if (!meta.hasEvaluated) continue
    for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
      const current = readDependencyValue(dep, ls, state, props)
      if (!dependencyEqual(dep, current, ls)) {
        meta.dirty = true
        if (typeof (global as any).__atomicDebug !== 'undefined') {
          ;(global as any).__atomicDebug(`invalidateLogic marks ${meta.name} dirty via ${dep.id}`)
        }
        if (meta.dirtyCause === null) {
          meta.dirtyCause = dep.id
        }
        break
      }
    }
  }
}""")
open(p,'w').write(s)
print("done")

new_derived = """function createDerivedProxy(raw: any, name: string, ls: AtomicLogicState): any {
  return createDerivedLeafProxy(raw, name, [], ls)
}

function createDerivedLeafProxy(raw: any, name: string, baseSegments: DepSegment[], ls: AtomicLogicState): any {
  raw = unwrapProxy(raw)
  if (!isTrackableObject(raw)) return raw
  if (Array.isArray(raw)) {
    return new Proxy(raw, {
      get(target: any, prop: string | symbol, receiver: any): any {
        if (prop === RAW) return target
        if (typeof prop === 'symbol') {
          if (prop === Symbol.iterator) {
            recordSelector(ls, name, target)
          }
          return Reflect.get(target, prop, target)
        }
        const idx = isArrayIndex(String(prop))
        if (idx !== null) {
          const segments = [...baseSegments, segIndex(idx)]
          recordSelectorLeaf(ls, name, segments, target[idx])
          return createDerivedLeafProxy(target[idx], name, segments, ls)
        }
        const segments = [...baseSegments, { kind: 'prop', key: prop }]
        if (typeof target[prop] === 'function') {
          recordSelector(ls, name, target)
          return Reflect.get(target, prop, target)
        }
        recordSelectorLeaf(ls, name, segments, target[prop])
        return createDerivedLeafProxy(target[prop], name, segments, ls)
      },
      has(target: any, prop: string | symbol): boolean {
        recordSelector(ls, name, target)
        return Reflect.has(target, prop)
      },
      ownKeys(target: any): ArrayLike<string | symbol> {
        recordSelector(ls, name, target)
        return Reflect.ownKeys(target)
      },
      getOwnPropertyDescriptor(target: any, prop: string | symbol): PropertyDescriptor | undefined {
        recordSelector(ls, name, target)
        return Reflect.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(target, prop)
      },
    })
  }
  return new Proxy(raw, {
    get(target: any, prop: string | symbol, receiver: any): any {
      if (prop === RAW) return target
      if (typeof prop === 'symbol') {
        recordSelector(ls, name, target)
        return Reflect.get(target, prop, target)
      }
      const segments = [...baseSegments, { kind: 'prop', key: prop }]
      if (typeof target[prop] === 'function') {
        recordSelector(ls, name, target)
        return Reflect.get(target, prop, target)
      }
      recordSelectorLeaf(ls, name, segments, target[prop])
      return createDerivedLeafProxy(target[prop], name, segments, ls)
    },
    has(target: any, prop: string | symbol): boolean {
      recordSelector(ls, name, target)
      return Reflect.has(target, prop)
    },
    ownKeys(target: any): ArrayLike<string | symbol> {
      recordSelector(ls, name, target)
      return Reflect.ownKeys(target)
    },
    getOwnPropertyDescriptor(target: any, prop: string | symbol): PropertyDescriptor | undefined {
      recordSelector(ls, name, target)
      return Reflect.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(target, prop)
    },
  })
}

function createForeignProxy(raw: any, name: string, ls: AtomicLogicState): any {
  return createDerivedLeafProxy(raw, name, [], ls)
}"""

cd /app && python3 - <<'EOF'
p='src/core/atomic-selectors.ts'
s=open(p).read()

# Fix getLocalAtomicValue session restore
s=s.replace("""function getLocalAtomicValue(ls: AtomicLogicState, name: string, state: any, props: any): any {
  const meta = ls.metas[name]
  if (meta.hasEvaluated && !meta.dirty) return meta.proxy
  const existing = ls.recomputeSession
  const session = existing && existing.state === state ? existing : createSession(ls, state, props)
  evaluateDirty(ls, name, session)
  if (existing === null || existing.state !== state) {
    ls.recomputeSession = null
  }
  return ls.metas[name].proxy
}""", """function getLocalAtomicValue(ls: AtomicLogicState, name: string, state: any, props: any): any {
  const meta = ls.metas[name]
  if (meta.hasEvaluated && !meta.dirty) return meta.proxy
  const existing = ls.recomputeSession
  const useExisting = existing !== null && existing.state === state
  const session = useExisting ? existing : createSession(ls, state, props)
  evaluateDirty(ls, name, session)
  if (!useExisting) {
    ls.recomputeSession = existing
  }
  return ls.metas[name].proxy
}""")

# Fix recompute session restore
s=s.replace("""function recompute(ls: AtomicLogicState, state: any, props: any): void {
  const existing = ls.recomputeSession
  const nested = existing !== null && existing.state === state
  const session = nested ? existing : createSession(ls, state, props)

  const order = buildTopologicalOrder(ls)
  for (const name of order) {
    evaluateDirty(ls, name, session)
  }

  if (!nested) {
    ls.recomputeSession = null
    for (const meta of Object.values(ls.metas)) {
      if (meta.dirty) {
        throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
      }
    }
  }
}""", """function recompute(ls: AtomicLogicState, state: any, props: any): void {
  const previous = ls.recomputeSession
  const nested = previous !== null && previous.state === state
  const session = nested ? previous : createSession(ls, state, props)

  const order = buildTopologicalOrder(ls)
  for (const name of order) {
    evaluateDirty(ls, name, session)
  }

  if (!nested) {
    ls.recomputeSession = previous
    for (const meta of Object.values(ls.metas)) {
      if (meta.dirty) {
        throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
      }
    }
  }
}""")
open(p,'w').write(s)
print("done")

cd /app && cat > test/jest/atomic-integration.js <<'EOF'
import { kea, resetContext, actions, reducers, selectors, connect } from '../../src'

describe('atomic selectors integration', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    resetContext({ atomicSelectors: true, createStore: true })
  })

  test('connected logic values work and propagate', () => {
    const otherLogic = kea([
      actions({ setValue: (value) => ({ value }) }),
      reducers({ value: ['x', { setValue: (_, { value }) => value }] }),
      selectors({
        upper: [(s) => [s.value], (value) => value.toUpperCase()],
      }),
    ])

    const logic = kea([
      connect({
        values: [otherLogic, ['upper as connectedUpper']],
      }),
      selectors({
        doubled: [(s) => [s.connectedUpper], (upper) => upper + upper],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.doubled).toBe('XX')

    otherLogic.actions.setValue('y')
    expect(logic.values.doubled).toBe('YY')

    const health = logic.selectorHealth()
    expect(health.selectors.doubled.dependencies).toContain('connectedUpper')

    unmount()
  })

  test('array index reads use list.N dependencies', () => {
    let runs = 0
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setList: (list) => ({ list }) }),
      reducers({ list: [['a', 'b'], { setList: (_, { list }) => list }] }),
      selectors({
        first: [
          (s) => [s.list],
          (list) => {
            runs += 1
            return list[0]
          },
        ],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.first).toBe('a')
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.first.dependencies).toContain('list.0')

    logic.actions.setList(['a', 'Z'])
    expect(logic.values.first).toBe('a')
    expect(runs).toBe(1)

    logic.actions.setList(['b', 'Z'])
    expect(logic.values.first).toBe('b')
    expect(runs).toBe(2)

    unmount()
  })

  test('lifecycle ordering is preserved with atomic selectors', () => {
    const order = []
    const logic = kea({
      events: () => ({
        beforeMount() { order.push('logic.beforeMount') },
        afterMount() { order.push('logic.afterMount') },
        beforeUnmount() { order.push('logic.beforeUnmount') },
        afterUnmount() { order.push('logic.afterUnmount') },
      }),
      selectors: {
        constant: [() => [], () => 42],
      },
    })

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.constant).toBe(42)
    expect(order).toEqual(['logic.beforeMount', 'logic.afterMount'])

    unmount()
    expect(order).toEqual([
      'logic.beforeMount',
      'logic.afterMount',
      'logic.beforeUnmount',
      'logic.afterUnmount',
    ])
  })

  test('props dependencies invalidate on props change', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      selectors({
        doubledProp: [(s, p) => [p.value], (value) => value * 2],
      }),
    ])

    const built = logic({ value: 2 })
    const unmount = built.mount()
    expect(built.values.doubledProp).toBe(4)
    expect(built.selectorHealth().selectors.doubledProp.dependencies).toContain('props:value')

    built.props.value = 3
    built.lastProps = { value: 3 }
    built.events.propsChanged?.({ value: 3 }, { value: 2 })
    expect(built.values.doubledProp).toBe(6)

    unmount()
  })

  test('multiple selectors depending on same state each evaluate once per action', () => {
    const runs = { a: 0, b: 0 }
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ set: (x) => ({ x }) }),
      reducers({ x: [1, { set: (_, { x }) => x }] }),
      selectors({
        selA: [(s) => [s.x], (x) => { runs.a += 1; return x * 2 }],
        selB: [(s) => [s.x], (x) => { runs.b += 1; return x * 3 }],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    logic.values.selA
    logic.values.selB
    expect(runs).toEqual({ a: 1, b: 1 })

    logic.actions.set(2)
    logic.values.selA
    logic.values.selB
    expect(runs).toEqual({ a: 2, b: 2 })

    unmount()
  })
})
EOF

STEP 77 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cd /app && python3 - <<'EOF'
p='src/core/atomic-selectors.ts'
s=open(p).read()

s=s.replace("""  if (state !== ls.lastState || props !== ls.lastProps) {
    invalidateLogic(ls, state, props)
  }
  const meta = ls.metas[name]""", """  invalidateLogic(ls, state, props)
  const meta = ls.metas[name]""")

s=s.replace("""function invalidateLogic(ls: AtomicLogicState, state: any, props: any): void {
  if (state === ls.lastState && props === ls.lastProps) return
  ls.lastState = state
  ls.lastProps = props
  for (const meta of Object.values(ls.metas)) {
    if (!meta.hasEvaluated) continue
    for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
      const current = readDependencyValue(dep, ls, state, props)
      if (!dependencyEqual(dep, current, ls)) {
        meta.dirty = true
        if (meta.dirtyCause === null) {
          meta.dirtyCause = dep.id
        }
        break
      }
    }
  }
}""", """function invalidateLogic(ls: AtomicLogicState, state: any, props: any): void {
  const stateSame = state === ls.lastState
  const propsSame = props === ls.lastProps
  if (stateSame && propsSame) {
    // Props may be mutated in place, so always re-check props dependencies.
    for (const meta of Object.values(ls.metas)) {
      if (!meta.hasEvaluated || meta.dirty) continue
      for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
        if (dep.type !== 'props') continue
        const current = readDependencyValue(dep, ls, state, props)
        if (!Object.is(dep.value, current)) {
          meta.dirty = true
          if (meta.dirtyCause === null) meta.dirtyCause = dep.id
          break
        }
      }
    }
    return
  }
  ls.lastState = state
  ls.lastProps = props
  for (const meta of Object.values(ls.metas)) {
    if (!meta.hasEvaluated) continue
    for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
      const current = readDependencyValue(dep, ls, state, props)
      if (!dependencyEqual(dep, current, ls)) {
        meta.dirty = true
        if (meta.dirtyCause === null) {
          meta.dirtyCause = dep.id
        }
        break
      }
    }
  }
}""")
open(p,'w').write(s)
print("done")
EOF

cd /app && cat > test/jest/atomic-react.js <<'EOF'
import React from 'react'
import { create, act } from 'react-test-renderer'
import { kea, resetContext, actions, reducers, selectors, Provider, useSelector } from '../../src'

describe('atomic selectors react', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    resetContext({ atomicSelectors: true, createStore: true })
  })

  test('components re-render only when accessed selector changes', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setUserName: (name) => ({ name }), setUserAge: (age) => ({ age }) }),
      reducers({
        user: [
          { name: 'chirpy', age: 30 },
          {
            setUserName: (state, { name }) => ({ ...state, name }),
            setUserAge: (state, { age }) => ({ ...state, age }),
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        userName: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.name],
        userAge: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.age],
      }),
    ])

    let renders = 0
    const Component = () => {
      renders += 1
      const name = useSelector(logic.selectors.userName)
      return <div>{name}</div>
    }

    const built = logic()
    built.mount()
    let tree
    act(() => {
      tree = create(<Provider><Component /></Provider>)
    })

    expect(tree.root.findByType('div').children[0]).toBe('chirpy')
    const renderCountAfterMount = renders
    expect(renderCountAfterMount).toBeGreaterThan(0)

    // age change should not re-render
    act(() => {
      logic.actions.setUserAge(31)
    })
    expect(renders).toBe(renderCountAfterMount)

    // name change should re-render
    act(() => {
      logic.actions.setUserName('pollo')
    })
    expect(tree.root.findByType('div').children[0]).toBe('pollo')
    expect(renders).toBe(renderCountAfterMount + 1)

    built.unmount()
  })
})
EOF
