cd /app && rm -f test/jest/atomic-react.js && cat > test/jest/atomic-extra.js <<'EOF'
import { kea, resetContext, actions, reducers, selectors, connect } from '../../src'

describe('atomic selectors extra', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    resetContext({ atomicSelectors: true, createStore: true })
  })

  test('resultEqualityCheck is respected for propagation', () => {
    const runs = { derived: 0, downstream: 0 }
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ set: (list) => ({ list }) }),
      reducers({ list: [['a', 'b'], { set: (_, { list }) => list }] }),
      selectors({
        lengthOnly: [
          (s) => [s.list],
          (list) => {
            runs.derived += 1
            return list.length
          },
          { resultEqualityCheck: (a, b) => a === b },
        ],
        downstream: [
          (s) => [s.lengthOnly],
          (length) => {
            runs.downstream += 1
            return length * 2
          },
        ],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.downstream).toBe(4)
    expect(runs).toEqual({ derived: 1, downstream: 1 })

    // change list but not length -> derived should not recompute
    logic.actions.set(['a', 'c'])
    expect(logic.values.downstream).toBe(4)
    expect(runs).toEqual({ derived: 1, downstream: 1 })

    // change length -> recompute
    logic.actions.set(['a', 'b', 'c'])
    expect(logic.values.downstream).toBe(6)
    expect(runs).toEqual({ derived: 2, downstream: 2 })

    unmount()
  })

  test('logic.selector returns the logic root', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      reducers({ a: ['x', {}] }),
    ])
    const unmount = logic.mount()
    const root = logic.selector()
    expect(root.a).toBe('x')
    unmount()
  })

  test('connect with wildcard values works', () => {
    const other = kea([
      reducers({ a: ['x', {}], b: ['y', {}] }),
    ])
    const logic = kea([
      connect({ values: [other, ['* as everything']] }),
      selectors({
        joined: [(s) => [s.everything], (everything) => `${everything.a}${everything.b}`],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.joined).toBe('xy')
    unmount()
  })

  test('Map iteration tracks all keys', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setData: (map) => ({ map }) }),
      reducers({
        data: [new Map([['a', 1], ['b', 2]]), { setData: (_, { map }) => map }],
      }),
      selectors({
        total: [
          (s) => [s.data],
          (data) => {
            let sum = 0
            data.forEach((v) => { sum += v })
            return sum
          },
        ],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.total).toBe(3)
    const health = logic.selectorHealth()
    expect(health.selectors.total.dependencies).toContain('data.map:a')
    expect(health.selectors.total.dependencies).toContain('data.map:b')

    logic.actions.setData(new Map([['a', 5], ['b', 2]]))
    expect(logic.values.total).toBe(7)
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.total.evaluations).toBe(2)

    unmount()
  })

  test('Set iteration tracks all values', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setData: (set) => ({ set }) }),
      reducers({
        data: [new Set([1, 2, 3]), { setData: (_, { set }) => set }],
      }),
      selectors({
        sum: [
          (s) => [s.data],
          (data) => {
            let total = 0
            for (const v of data) total += v
            return total
          },
        ],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.sum).toBe(6)
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.sum.dependencies).toContain('data.set:1')
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.sum.dependencies).toContain('data.set:2')
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.sum.dependencies).toContain('data.set:3')

    logic.actions.setData(new Set([1, 2, 4]))
    expect(logic.values.sum).toBe(7)

    unmount()
  })

  test('Array map tracks all indices', () => {
    let runs = 0
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setList: (list) => ({ list }) }),
      reducers({ list: [[1, 2, 3], { setList: (_, { list }) => list }] }),
      selectors({
        doubled: [
          (s) => [s.list],
          (list) => {
            runs += 1
            return list.map((x) => x * 2)
          },
        ],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.doubled).toEqual([2, 4, 6])
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.doubled.dependencies).toContain('list.0')
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.doubled.dependencies).toContain('list.2')

    logic.actions.setList([1, 2, 4])
    expect(logic.values.doubled).toEqual([2, 4, 8])
    expect(runs).toBe(2)

    unmount()
  })

  test('mount also throws on circular dependencies', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      selectors({
        a: [(s) => [s.b], (b) => b],
        b: [(s) => [s.a], (a) => a],
      }),
    ])
    expect(() => logic.mount()).toThrow('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
  })

  test('health stable identity persists through wrapping', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ set: (x) => ({ x }) }),
      reducers({ x: [1, { set: (_, { x }) => x }] }),
      selectors({
        doubleX: [(s) => [s.x], (x) => x * 2],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    logic.values.doubleX
    const health1 = logic.selectorHealth()
    expect(health1.selectors.doubleX).toBeDefined()
    expect(health1.selectors.x).toBeDefined()

    logic.actions.set(2)
    const health2 = logic.selectorHealth()
    // Same identity - health persists through selector function wrapping
    expect(health2.selectors.doubleX.evaluations).toBe(2)
    unmount()
  })
})

cd /app && cat > test/jest/atomic-edge.js <<'EOF'
import { kea, resetContext, actions, reducers, selectors, activatePlugin } from '../../src'

describe('atomic selectors edge cases', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    resetContext({ atomicSelectors: true, createStore: true })
  })

  test('dependencies list only leaf paths, not parent nodes', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      reducers({ user: [{ name: 'x', age: 1 }, {}] }),
      selectors({
        userName: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.name],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    logic.values.userName
    const deps = logic.selectorHealth().selectors.userName.dependencies
    expect(deps).toContain('user.name')
    expect(deps).not.toContain('user')
    unmount()
  })

  test('direct selector calls return values through proxies', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      reducers({ user: [{ name: 'x', age: 1 }, {}] }),
      selectors({
        userName: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.name],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.selectors.user()).toEqual({ name: 'x', age: 1 })
    expect(logic.selectors.userName()).toBe('x')
    unmount()
  })

  test('includes with no match tracks all indices', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setList: (list) => ({ list }) }),
      reducers({ list: [['a', 'b'], { setList: (_, { list }) => list }] }),
      selectors({
        hasZ: [(s) => [s.list], (list) => list.includes('z')],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.hasZ).toBe(false)
    const deps = logic.selectorHealth().selectors.hasZ.dependencies
    expect(deps).toContain('list.0')
    expect(deps).toContain('list.1')

    logic.actions.setList(['a', 'Z'])
    expect(logic.values.hasZ).toBe(false)
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.hasZ.evaluations).toBe(2)

    unmount()
  })

  test('plugin afterMount ordering preserved with atomic engine', () => {
    const order = []
    activatePlugin({
      name: 'ordering-test',
      events: {
        afterMount(logic) { order.push('plugin.afterMount') },
        beforeMount(logic) { order.push('plugin.beforeMount') },
        afterUnmount(logic) { order.push('plugin.afterUnmount') },
        beforeUnmount(logic) { order.push('plugin.beforeUnmount') },
      },
    })

    const logic = kea({
      events: {
        beforeMount() { order.push('logic.beforeMount') },
        afterMount() { order.push('logic.afterMount') },
        beforeUnmount() { order.push('logic.beforeUnmount') },
        afterUnmount() { order.push('logic.afterUnmount') },
      },
    })

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(order).toEqual(['plugin.beforeMount', 'logic.beforeMount', 'plugin.afterMount', 'logic.afterMount'])
    unmount()
    expect(order).toEqual([
      'plugin.beforeMount',
      'logic.beforeMount',
      'plugin.afterMount',
      'logic.afterMount',
      'plugin.beforeUnmount',
      'logic.beforeUnmount',
      'plugin.afterUnmount',
      'logic.afterUnmount',
    ])
  })

  test('dirtyCause persists after recompute', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setName: (name) => ({ name }) }),
      reducers({ name: ['x', { setName: (_, { name }) => name }] }),
      selectors({
        upper: [(s) => [s.name], (name) => name.toUpperCase()],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    logic.values.upper
    logic.actions.setName('y')
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.upper.dirtyCause).toBe('name')
    logic.values.upper
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.upper.dirtyCause).toBe('name')
    unmount()
  })
})
EOF

cd /app && cat > test/jest/atomic-snapshot.js <<'EOF'
import { kea, resetContext, actions, reducers, selectors, getContext } from '../../src'

describe('atomic selectors snapshot stability (React integration contract)', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    resetContext({ atomicSelectors: true, createStore: true })
  })

  test('snapshots are stable unless the accessed selector changes', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setUserName: (name) => ({ name }), setUserAge: (age) => ({ age }) }),
      reducers({
        user: [
          { name: 'chirpy', age: 30 },
          {
            setUserName: (state, { name }) => ({ ...state, name }),
            setUserAge: (state, { age }) => ({ ...state, age }),
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        userName: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.name],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    const store = getContext().store
    const snapshot = () => logic.selectors.userName(store.getState())

    const first = snapshot()
    expect(first).toBe('chirpy')

    // unrelated change -> same value (primitive string, so same by value)
    logic.actions.setUserAge(31)
    expect(snapshot()).toBe('chirpy')

    // related change -> new value
    logic.actions.setUserName('pollo')
    expect(snapshot()).toBe('pollo')

    // object selector: proxy reference should be stable across unrelated changes
    const userSnap1 = logic.selectors.user(store.getState())
    logic.actions.setUserAge(32)
    const userSnap2 = logic.selectors.user(store.getState())
    expect(userSnap2).not.toBe(userSnap1) // whole user object changed, so re-render
    expect(userSnap2.name).toBe('pollo')
    expect(userSnap2.age).toBe(32)

    unmount()
  })
})
EOF

cd /app && cat > test/jest/atomic-compat.js <<'EOF'
import { kea, resetContext, actions, reducers, selectors } from '../../src'

describe('atomic selectors compatibility with baseline behaviors', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    resetContext({ atomicSelectors: true, createStore: true })
  })

  test('selectors have the store and props as default', () => {
    const books = { 1: 'book1', 2: 'book2' }
    const logic = kea({
      reducers: () => ({ books: [books, {}], bookId: [1, {}] }),
      selectors: ({ selectors }) => ({
        book: [
          () => [selectors.books, selectors.bookId, (_, props) => props.extra],
          (books, bookId, extra) => books[bookId] + extra,
        ],
      }),
    })
    const logicWithProps = logic({ extra: 'nope' })
    const unmount = logicWithProps.mount()
    expect(logicWithProps.selectors.books()).toEqual(books)
    expect(logicWithProps.selectors.bookId()).toEqual(1)
    expect(logicWithProps.selectors.book()).toBe('book1nope')
    unmount()
  })

  test('selectors run only once when input has not changed', () => {
    const books = { 1: 'book1', 2: 'book2' }
    let selectorRan = 0
    const logic = kea({
      reducers: () => ({ books: [books, {}], bookId: [1, {}] }),
      selectors: ({ selectors }) => ({
        book: [
          () => [selectors.books, selectors.bookId],
          (books, bookId) => {
            selectorRan += 1
            return books[bookId]
          },
        ],
      }),
    })
    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.selectors.book()).toBe('book1')
    expect(selectorRan).toBe(1)
    expect(logic.selectors.book()).toBe('book1')
    expect(selectorRan).toBe(1)
    unmount()
  })

  test('cloning logic.values takes a snapshot', () => {
    const logic = kea({
      actions: () => ({ setBookId: (bookId) => ({ bookId }) }),
      reducers: ({ actions }) => ({
        books: [{ 1: 'book1', 2: 'book2' }, {}],
        bookId: [1, { [actions.setBookId]: (_, payload) => payload.bookId }],
      }),
      selectors: ({ selectors }) => ({
        book: [() => [selectors.books, selectors.bookId], (books, bookId) => books[bookId]],
      }),
    })
    const built = logic()
    const unmount = built.mount()
    expect(built.values.book).toBe('book1')
    const cloned = { ...built.values }
    built.actions.setBookId(2)
    expect(built.values.book).toBe('book2')
    expect(cloned.book).toBe('book1')
    unmount()
  })

  test('custom memoization resultEqualityCheck works', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ addValue: (value) => ({ value }), setValue: (index, value) => ({ index, value }) }),
      reducers({
        values: [
          [],
          {
            addValue: (state, { value }) => [...state, value],
            setValue: (state, { index, value }) => state.map((s, i) => (i === index ? value : s)),
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        reversedValues: [(s) => [s.values], (values) => [...values].reverse()],
        reversedValuesIfLengthChanges: [
          (s) => [s.values],
          (values) => [...values].reverse(),
          { resultEqualityCheck: (a, b) => a.length === b.length },
        ],
      }),
    ])
    logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.values).toEqual([])
    logic.actions.addValue('first')
    expect(logic.values.reversedValues).toEqual(['first'])
    expect(logic.values.reversedValuesIfLengthChanges).toEqual(['first'])
    logic.actions.addValue('second')
    expect(logic.values.reversedValues).toEqual(['second', 'first'])
    expect(logic.values.reversedValuesIfLengthChanges).toEqual(['second', 'first'])
    logic.actions.setValue(1, 'SECOND')
    expect(logic.values.values).toEqual(['first', 'SECOND'])
    expect(logic.values.reversedValues).toEqual(['SECOND', 'first'])
    expect(logic.values.reversedValuesIfLengthChanges).toEqual(['second', 'first'])
    logic.actions.addValue('third')
    expect(logic.values.reversedValues).toEqual(['third', 'SECOND', 'first'])
    expect(logic.values.reversedValuesIfLengthChanges).toEqual(['third', 'SECOND', 'first'])
  })
})
EOF

p='src/core/atomic-selectors.ts'
s=open(p).read()

# Add recordLength/recordSize helpers after recordProps
s=s.replace("""function recordProps(ls: AtomicLogicState, segments: DepSegment[], value: any): void {
  const t = activeTracker
  if (t && t.ls === ls) {
    t.addProps(segments, value)
  }
}""", """function recordProps(ls: AtomicLogicState, segments: DepSegment[], value: any): void {
  const t = activeTracker
  if (t && t.ls === ls) {
    t.addProps(segments, value)
  }
}

function recordCollectionSize(ls: AtomicLogicState, base: DepSegment[], target: any[] | Map<any, any> | Set<any>): void {
  const segment: DepSegment = { kind: 'prop', key: 'length' }
  if (Array.isArray(target)) {
    recordLeaf(ls, [...base, segment], target.length)
  } else {
    recordLeaf(ls, [...base, { kind: 'prop', key: 'size' }], target.size)
  }
}""")

# arrayIterator: record length
s=s.replace("""function arrayIterator(ls: AtomicLogicState, base: DepSegment[], target: any[], mode: 'values' | 'entries'): IterableIterator<any> {
  let i = 0
  return {
    next(): IteratorResult<any> {
      if (i < target.length) {""", """function arrayIterator(ls: AtomicLogicState, base: DepSegment[], target: any[], mode: 'values' | 'entries'): IterableIterator<any> {
  let i = 0
  recordCollectionSize(ls, base, target)
  return {
    next(): IteratorResult<any> {
      if (i < target.length) {""")

# In wrapArrayMethod: add recordCollectionSize at the top of each function that iterates all.
# Simplest: add recordCollectionSize(ls, base, target) at the start of every returned method,
# except includes/indexOf/lastIndexOf/at where we conditionally handle.

s=s.replace("""  switch (prop) {
    case 'includes':
      return (searchElement: any, fromIndex?: number) => {
        const start = fromIndex === undefined ? 0 : Math.max(fromIndex >= 0 ? fromIndex : target.length + fromIndex, 0)
        for (let i = start; i < target.length; i++) {
          recordIndex(i)
          if (Object.is(target[i], searchElement)) return true
        }
        return false
      }
    case 'indexOf':
      return (searchElement: any, fromIndex?: number) => {
        const start = fromIndex === undefined ? 0 : Math.max(fromIndex >= 0 ? fromIndex : target.length + fromIndex, 0)
        for (let i = start; i < target.length; i++) {
          recordIndex(i)
          if (target[i] === searchElement) return i
        }
        return -1
      }
    case 'lastIndexOf':
      return (searchElement: any, fromIndex?: number) => {
        const start =
          fromIndex === undefined
            ? target.length - 1
            : Math.min(fromIndex >= 0 ? fromIndex : target.length + fromIndex, target.length - 1)
        for (let i = start; i >= 0; i--) {
          recordIndex(i)
          if (target[i] === searchElement) return i
        }
        return -1
      }""", """  switch (prop) {
    case 'includes':
      return (searchElement: any, fromIndex?: number) => {
        const start = fromIndex === undefined ? 0 : Math.max(fromIndex >= 0 ? fromIndex : target.length + fromIndex, 0)
        for (let i = start; i < target.length; i++) {
          recordIndex(i)
          if (Object.is(target[i], searchElement)) return true
        }
        recordCollectionSize(ls, base, target)
        return false
      }
    case 'indexOf':
      return (searchElement: any, fromIndex?: number) => {
        const start = fromIndex === undefined ? 0 : Math.max(fromIndex >= 0 ? fromIndex : target.length + fromIndex, 0)
        for (let i = start; i < target.length; i++) {
          recordIndex(i)
          if (target[i] === searchElement) return i
        }
        recordCollectionSize(ls, base, target)
        return -1
      }
    case 'lastIndexOf':
      return (searchElement: any, fromIndex?: number) => {
        const start =
          fromIndex === undefined
            ? target.length - 1
            : Math.min(fromIndex >= 0 ? fromIndex : target.length + fromIndex, target.length - 1)
        for (let i = start; i >= 0; i--) {
          recordIndex(i)
          if (target[i] === searchElement) return i
        }
        recordCollectionSize(ls, base, target)
        return -1
      }""")

p='src/core/atomic-selectors.ts'
s=open(p).read()

s=s.replace("""function getLocalAtomicValue(ls: AtomicLogicState, name: string, state: any, props: any): any {
  const meta = ls.metas[name]
  if (meta.hasEvaluated && !meta.dirty) return meta.proxy
  const existing = ls.recomputeSession
  const useExisting = existing !== null && existing.state === state
  const session = useExisting ? existing : createSession(ls, state, props)
  evaluateDirty(ls, name, session)
  if (!useExisting) {
    ls.recomputeSession = existing
  }
  return ls.metas[name].proxy
}""", """function getLocalAtomicValue(ls: AtomicLogicState, name: string, state: any, props: any): any {
  const meta = ls.metas[name]
  if (meta.hasEvaluated && !meta.dirty) return meta.proxy
  const existing = ls.recomputeSession
  const useExisting = existing !== null && existing.state === state
  const session = useExisting ? existing : createSession(ls, state, props)
  try {
    evaluateDirty(ls, name, session)
  } finally {
    if (!useExisting) {
      ls.recomputeSession = existing
    }
  }
  return ls.metas[name].proxy
}""")

s=s.replace("""function recompute(ls: AtomicLogicState, state: any, props: any): void {
  const previous = ls.recomputeSession
  const nested = previous !== null && previous.state === state
  const session = nested ? previous : createSession(ls, state, props)

  const order = buildTopologicalOrder(ls)
  for (const name of order) {
    evaluateDirty(ls, name, session)
  }

  if (!nested) {
    ls.recomputeSession = previous
    for (const meta of Object.values(ls.metas)) {
      if (meta.dirty) {
        throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
      }
    }
  }
}""", """function recompute(ls: AtomicLogicState, state: any, props: any): void {
  const previous = ls.recomputeSession
  const nested = previous !== null && previous.state === state
  const session = nested ? previous : createSession(ls, state, props)

  try {
    const order = buildTopologicalOrder(ls)
    for (const name of order) {
      evaluateDirty(ls, name, session)
    }

    if (!nested) {
      for (const meta of Object.values(ls.metas)) {
        if (meta.dirty) {
          throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
        }
      }
    }
  } finally {
    if (!nested) {
      ls.recomputeSession = previous
    }
  }
}""")

cd /app && cat > test/jest/atomic-nested.js <<'EOF'
import { kea, resetContext, actions, reducers, selectors } from '../../src'

describe('atomic selectors nested paths', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    resetContext({ atomicSelectors: true, createStore: true })
  })

  test('nested object paths are tracked at leaf level', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setCity: (city) => ({ city }), setZip: (zip) => ({ zip }) }),
      reducers({
        user: [
          { address: { city: 'a', zip: 1 } },
          {
            setCity: (state, { city }) => ({ ...state, address: { ...state.address, city } }),
            setZip: (state, { zip }) => ({ ...state, address: { ...state.address, zip } }),
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        city: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.address.city],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.city).toBe('a')
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.city.dependencies).toContain('user.address.city')

    logic.actions.setZip(2)
    expect(logic.values.city).toBe('a')
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.city.evaluations).toBe(1)

    logic.actions.setCity('b')
    expect(logic.values.city).toBe('b')
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.city.evaluations).toBe(2)

    unmount()
  })

  test('Map value object leaf is tracked', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setMap: (map) => ({ map }) }),
      reducers({
        data: [new Map([['a', { name: 'x', age: 1 }]]), { setMap: (_, { map }) => map }],
      }),
      selectors({
        name: [(s) => [s.data], (data) => data.get('a').name],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.name).toBe('x')
    const deps = logic.selectorHealth().selectors.name.dependencies
    expect(deps).toContain('data.map:a')
    expect(deps).toContain('data.map:a.name')

    logic.actions.setMap(new Map([['a', { name: 'x', age: 2 }]]))
    expect(logic.values.name).toBe('x')
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.name.evaluations).toBe(1)

    logic.actions.setMap(new Map([['a', { name: 'y', age: 2 }]]))
    expect(logic.values.name).toBe('y')
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.name.evaluations).toBe(2)

    unmount()
  })
})

cd /app && python3 - <<'EOF'
p='src/core/atomic-selectors.ts'
s=open(p).read()

# Add pruneDependencies after segmentsToId
s=s.replace("""function segIndex(i: number): DepSegment {
  return { kind: 'index', key: i }
}""", """function segIndex(i: number): DepSegment {
  return { kind: 'index', key: i }
}

function isPrunablePrefix(dep: AtomicDependency): boolean {
  if (dep.type !== 'leaf' && dep.type !== 'props') return false
  if (!dep.segments || dep.segments.length === 0) return false
  const last = dep.segments[dep.segments.length - 1]
  return last.kind === 'prop' || last.kind === 'index'
}

function pruneDependencies(deps: AtomicDependency[]): AtomicDependency[] {
  const ids = deps.map((d) => d.id)
  return deps.filter((d) => {
    if (!isPrunablePrefix(d)) return true
    const prefix = d.id + '.'
    return !ids.some((other) => other !== d.id && other.startsWith(prefix))
  })
}""")

# Apply pruning in evaluateOne
s=s.replace("""  meta.dependencies = Array.from(tracker.deps.values())
  meta.evaluations += 1""", """  meta.dependencies = pruneDependencies(Array.from(tracker.deps.values()))
  meta.evaluations += 1""")
open(p,'w').write(s)
print("done")
EOF

COMMAND:
cd /app && python3 - <<'EOF'
p='src/core/atomic-selectors.ts'
s=open(p).read()

s=s.replace("""  const result = meta.computeFn ? meta.computeFn(...inputValues) : undefined

  // If the result is exactly one of the input values (identity selector),
  // record the base dependency so the whole object is tracked.
  for (let i = 0; i < inputValues.length; i++) {
    if (inputBases[i]) {
      const unwrappedInput = unwrapProxy(inputValues[i])
      if (result === inputValues[i] || (isTrackableObject(result) && unwrapProxy(result) === unwrappedInput)) {
        tracker.addDep(inputBases[i] as AtomicDependency)
      }
    }
  }

  return result
}""", """  const result = meta.computeFn ? meta.computeFn(...inputValues) : undefined

  // If the result is exactly one of the input values (identity selector),
  // record the base dependency so the whole object is tracked.
  for (let i = 0; i < inputValues.length; i++) {
    if (inputBases[i]) {
      const unwrappedInput = unwrapProxy(inputValues[i])
      if (result === inputValues[i] || (isTrackableObject(result) && unwrapProxy(result) === unwrappedInput)) {
        tracker.addDep(inputBases[i] as AtomicDependency)
      }
    }
  }

  // If any input proxy is embedded inside the result (e.g. `{ user }`), we
  // still depend on the whole input value.
  if (isTrackableObject(result)) {
    const visited = new Set<any>()
    let stack: any[] = [result]
    for (let depth = 0; stack.length > 0 && depth < 6; depth++) {
      const next: any[] = []
      for (const item of stack) {
        if (visited.has(item)) continue
        visited.add(item)
        const raw = unwrapProxy(item)
        if (!isTrackableObject(raw)) continue
        for (let i = 0; i < inputValues.length; i++) {
          if (inputBases[i] && (item === inputValues[i] || raw === unwrapProxy(inputValues[i]))) {
            tracker.addDep(inputBases[i] as AtomicDependency)
          }
        }
        if (Array.isArray(raw)) {
          for (const v of raw) if (isTrackableObject(v)) next.push(v)
        } else if (raw instanceof Map) {
          for (const v of raw.values()) if (isTrackableObject(v)) next.push(v)
        } else if (raw instanceof Set) {
          for (const v of raw.values()) if (isTrackableObject(v)) next.push(v)
        } else {
          for (const key of Object.keys(raw)) {
            const v = raw[key]
            if (isTrackableObject(v)) next.push(v)
          }
        }
      }
      stack = next
    }
  }

  return result
}""")
open(p,'w').write(s)
print("done")
EOF
npx tsc --noEmit 2>&1 | head -5 && cat > test/jest/atomic-embed.js <<'EOF'
import { kea, resetContext, actions, reducers, selectors } from '../../src'

describe('atomic selectors embedded inputs', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    resetContext({ atomicSelectors: true, createStore: true })
  })

  test('selector embedding an object input re-evaluates when it changes', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setAge: (age) => ({ age }) }),
      reducers({
        user: [
          { name: 'x', age: 1 },
          { setAge: (state, { age }) => ({ ...state, age }) },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        wrapped: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => ({ user })],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.wrapped.user.name).toBe('x')
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.wrapped.dependencies).toContain('user')

    logic.actions.setAge(2)
    expect(logic.values.wrapped.user.age).toBe(2)
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.wrapped.evaluations).toBe(2)

    unmount()
  })

  test('nested embedded input works', () => {
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setName: (name) => ({ name }) }),
      reducers({
        user: [
          { name: 'x', age: 1 },
          { setName: (state, { name }) => ({ ...state, name }) },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        nested: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => ({ data: { user } })],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.nested.data.user.name).toBe('x')
    logic.actions.setName('y')
    expect(logic.values.nested.data.user.name).toBe('y')
    unmount()
  })
})
EOF

import { kea, resetContext, actions, reducers, selectors } from '../../src'

describe('atomic selectors derived nested', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    resetContext({ atomicSelectors: true, createStore: true })
  })

  test('derived nested leaf tracking prunes intermediate parents', () => {
    let level2Runs = 0
    const logic = kea([
      actions({ setCity: (city) => ({ city }), setZip: (zip) => ({ zip }) }),
      reducers({
        user: [
          { address: { city: 'a', zip: 1 } },
          {
            setCity: (state, { city }) => ({ ...state, address: { ...state.address, city } }),
            setZip: (state, { zip }) => ({ ...state, address: { ...state.address, zip } }),
          },
        ],
      }),
      selectors({
        address: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.address],
        city: [
          (s) => [s.address],
          (address) => {
            level2Runs += 1
            return address.city
          },
        ],
      }),
    ])

    const unmount = logic.mount()
    expect(logic.values.city).toBe('a')
    expect(level2Runs).toBe(1)
    expect(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.city.dependencies).toEqual(['address.city'])

    logic.actions.setZip(2)
    expect(logic.values.city).toBe('a')
    expect(level2Runs).toBe(1)

    logic.actions.setCity('b')
    expect(logic.values.city).toBe('b')
    expect(level2Runs).toBe(2)

    unmount()
  })
})
EOF

import { kea, resetContext, actions, reducers, selectors } from '../../src'

test('debug derived nested', () => {
  global.__atomicDebug = (msg) => console.log('ATOMIC:', msg)
  resetContext({ atomicSelectors: true, createStore: true })
  const logic = kea([
    actions({ setCity: (city) => ({ city }), setZip: (zip) => ({ zip }) }),
    reducers({
      user: [
        { address: { city: 'a', zip: 1 } },
        {
          setCity: (state, { city }) => ({ ...state, address: { ...state.address, city } }),
          setZip: (state, { zip }) => ({ ...state, address: { ...state.address, zip } }),
        },
      ],
    }),
    selectors({
      address: [(s) => [s.user], (user) => user.address],
      city: [(s) => [s.address], (address) => address.city],
    }),
  ])
  const unmount = logic.mount()
  console.log('city', logic.values.city)
  console.log('health', JSON.stringify(logic.selectorHealth().selectors.city))

  test('reducer selectors appear in selectorHealth', () => {
    const logic = kea({
      reducers: { value: ['x', {}] },
    })
    const unmount = logic.mount()
    logic.values.value
    const health = logic.selectorHealth()
    expect(health.selectors.value).toBeDefined()
    expect(health.topologicalOrder).toEqual(['value'])
    unmount()
  })""")

    14	/**
    15	 * Atomic Signal Selector Engine
    16	 *
    17	 * When enabled through `resetContext({ atomicSelectors: true })` Kea tracks
    18	 * selector dependencies at the exact leaf level (e.g. `user.name`), supports
    19	 * fine-grained access into Maps, Sets and Arrays, propagates changes only to
    20	 * affected selectors and exposes `logic.selectorHealth()`.
    21	 */

    29	type DepSegment =
    30	  | { kind: 'prop'; key: string | number }
    31	  | { kind: 'index'; key: number }
    32	  | { kind: 'mapKey'; key: any }
    33	  | { kind: 'setValue'; value: any }

    35	interface AtomicDependency {
    36	  id: string
    37	  type: 'leaf' | 'selector' | 'props'
    38	  segments?: DepSegment[]
    39	  selectorName?: string

    41	  value: any
    42	  explicit?: boolean

   940	function registerAtomicSelector(
   941	  ls: AtomicLogicState,
   942	  name: string,
   943	  input: Partial<AtomicSelectorMeta>,
   944	): AtomicSelectorMeta {
   945	  const meta: AtomicSelectorMeta = {
   946	    name,
   947	    rootDerived: false,
   948	    value: undefined,
   949	    proxy: undefined,
   950	    dependencies: [],
   951	    dependents: [],

   953	    dirtyCause: null,
   954	    dirty: false,
   955	    hasEvaluated: false,

   962	function makeAtomicSelectorFn(ls: AtomicLogicState, name: string, rootDerived: boolean, getter: Selector): AtomicSelectorFn {
   963	  const fn = ((state: any = getStoreState(), props: any = ls.logic.props) => getter(state, props)) as AtomicSelectorFn
   964	  fn._keaAtomicMeta = { logic: ls.logic, name, rootDerived }
   965	  return fn
   966	}
   967	
   968	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   969	// Evaluation
   970	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   971	
   972	function evaluateAtomicSelector(ls: AtomicLogicState, name: string, state: any, props: any): any {
   973	  if (state === undefined) state = getStoreState()
   974	  if (props === undefined) props = ls.logic.props
   975	  invalidateLogic(ls, state, props)
   976	  const meta = ls.metas[name]
   977	  if (!meta) {
   978	    throw new Error(`[KEA] Atomic selector "${name}" not found in logic "${ls.logic.pathString}"`)
   979	  }
   980	  const hasDirty = Object.values(ls.metas).some((m) => m.dirty)
   981	  if (meta.hasEvaluated && !meta.dirty && !hasDirty) {
   982	    return meta.proxy
   983	  }
   984	  recompute(ls, state, props)
   985	  return ls.metas[name].proxy
   986	}
   987	
   988	function invalidateLogic(ls: AtomicLogicState, state: any, props: any): void {
   989	  const stateSame = state === ls.lastState
   990	  const propsSame = props === ls.lastProps
   991	  if (stateSame && propsSame) {
   992	    // Props may be mutated in place, so always re-check props dependencies.
   993	    for (const meta of Object.values(ls.metas)) {
   994	      if (!meta.hasEvaluated || meta.dirty) continue
   995	      for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
   996	        if (dep.type !== 'props') continue
   997	        const current = readDependencyValue(dep, ls, state, props)
   998	        if (!Object.is(dep.value, current)) {
   999	          meta.dirty = true
  1000	          if (meta.dirtyCause === null) meta.dirtyCause = dep.id
  1001	          break
  1002	        }
  1003	      }
  1004	    }
  1005	    return
  1006	  }
  1007	  ls.lastState = state
  1008	  ls.lastProps = props
  1009	  for (const meta of Object.values(ls.metas)) {
  1010	    if (!meta.hasEvaluated) continue
  1011	    for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
  1012	      const current = readDependencyValue(dep, ls, state, props)
  1013	      if (!dependencyEqual(dep, current, ls)) {
  1014	        meta.dirty = true
  1015	        if (meta.dirtyCause === null) {
  1016	          meta.dirtyCause = dep.id
  1017	        }
  1018	        break
  1019	      }
  1020	    }
  1021	  }
  1022	}
  1023	
  1024	function createSession(ls: AtomicLogicState, state: any, props: any): RecomputeSession {
  1025	  for (const meta of Object.values(ls.metas)) {
  1026	    if (!meta.hasEvaluated) meta.dirty = true
  1027	  }
  1028	  const session: RecomputeSession = { processed: new Set(), visiting: new Set(), state, props }
  1029	  ls.recomputeSession = session
  1030	  return session
  1031	}
  1032	
  1033	function recompute(ls: AtomicLogicState, state: any, props: any): void {
  1034	  const previous = ls.recomputeSession
  1035	  const nested = previous !== null && previous.state === state
  1036	  const session = nested ? previous : createSession(ls, state, props)
  1037	
  1038	  try {
  1039	    const order = buildTopologicalOrder(ls)
  1040	    for (const name of order) {
  1041	      evaluateDirty(ls, name, session)
  1042	    }
  1043	
  1044	    if (!nested) {
  1045	      for (const meta of Object.values(ls.metas)) {
  1046	        if (meta.dirty) {
  1047	          throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
  1048	        }
  1049	      }
  1050	    }
  1051	  } finally {
  1052	    if (!nested) {
  1053	      ls.recomputeSession = previous
  1054	    }
  1055	  }
  1056	}
  1057	
  1058	function evaluateDirty(ls: AtomicLogicState, name: string, session: RecomputeSession): void {
  1059	  const meta = ls.metas[name]
  1060	  if (!meta) return
  1061	  if (!meta.dirty || session.processed.has(name)) return
  1062	  if (session.visiting.has(name)) {
  1063	    throw new Error('[KEA] Circular dependency detected')
  1064	  }
  1065	  session.visiting.add(name)
  1066	  try {
  1067	    // evaluate local selector dependencies first
  1068	    for (const dep of meta.dependencies) {
  1069	      if (dep.type === 'selector' && !dep.foreignFn && dep.selectorName && ls.metas[dep.selectorName]?.dirty) {
  1070	        evaluateDirty(ls, dep.selectorName, session)
  1071	      }
  1072	    }
  1073	    if (session.processed.has(name)) return
  1074	    if (!meta.dirty) return
  1075	    evaluateOne(ls, name, session.state, session.props)
  1076	    session.processed.add(name)
  1077	  } finally {
  1078	    session.visiting.delete(name)
  1079	  }
  1080	}

  1082	function getLocalAtomicValue(ls: AtomicLogicState, name: string, state: any, props: any): any {
  1083	  const meta = ls.metas[name]
  1084	  if (meta.hasEvaluated && !meta.dirty) return meta.proxy
  1085	  const existing = ls.recomputeSession
  1086	  const useExisting = existing !== null && existing.state === state
  1087	  const session = useExisting ? existing : createSession(ls, state, props)
  1088	  try {
  1089	    evaluateDirty(ls, name, session)
  1090	  } finally {
  1091	    if (!useExisting) {
  1092	      ls.recomputeSession = existing
  1093	    }
  1094	  }
  1095	  return ls.metas[name].proxy
  1096	}
  1097	
  1098	function evaluateOne(ls: AtomicLogicState, name: string, state: any, props: any): void {
  1099	  const meta = ls.metas[name]
  1100	  const oldValue = meta.value
  1101	  const hadEvaluated = meta.hasEvaluated
  1102	
  1103	  const tracker = new Tracker(ls)
  1104	  const previousTracker = activeTracker
  1105	  activeTracker = tracker
  1106	  let rawResult: any
  1107	  try {
  1108	    if (meta.rootDerived) {
  1109	      const rootRaw = getLogicRootRaw(ls.logic, state)
  1110	      const key = meta.reducerKey || name
  1111	      const raw = rootRaw == null ? undefined : rootRaw[key]
  1112	      tracker.addLeaf([{ kind: 'prop', key }], raw)
  1113	      rawResult = raw
  1114	    } else {
  1115	      rawResult = evaluateCompute(ls, meta, state, props, tracker)
  1116	    }
  1117	  } finally {
  1118	    activeTracker = previousTracker
  1119	  }
  1120	
  1121	  const newRaw = unwrapProxy(rawResult)
  1122	  const resultUnchanged = hadEvaluated && sameValue(oldValue, newRaw, meta.memoizeOptions)
  1123	  meta.value = resultUnchanged ? oldValue : newRaw
  1124	  meta.proxy = meta.rootDerived
  1125	    ? createStateProxy(meta.value, meta.reducerKey ? [{ kind: 'prop', key: meta.reducerKey }] : [], ls)
  1126	    : createDerivedProxy(meta.value, name, ls)
  1127	  meta.dependencies = pruneDependencies(Array.from(tracker.deps.values()))
  1128	  meta.evaluations += 1
  1129	  meta.hasEvaluated = true
  1130	  meta.dirty = false
  1131	
  1132	  rebuildDependents(ls)
  1133	
  1134	  if (resultUnchanged) {
  1135	    return
  1136	  }
  1137	
  1138	  for (const depName of meta.dependents) {
  1139	    const depMeta = ls.metas[depName]
  1140	    if (!depMeta || depMeta.dirty) continue
  1141	    let changed = false
  1142	    for (const d of depMeta.dependencies) {
  1143	      if (d.type !== 'selector' || d.selectorName !== name) continue
  1144	      if (d.segments && d.segments.length > 0) {
  1145	        if (!Object.is(readValueBySegments(oldValue, d.segments), readValueBySegments(newRaw, d.segments))) {
  1146	          changed = true
  1147	          break
  1148	        }
  1149	      } else if (!hadEvaluated || !sameValue(oldValue, newRaw, meta.memoizeOptions)) {
  1150	        changed = true
  1151	        break
  1152	      }
  1153	    }
  1154	    if (changed) {
  1155	      depMeta.dirty = true
  1156	      depMeta.dirtyCause = `selector:${name}`
  1157	    }
  1158	  }
  1159	}
  1160	
  1161	function evaluateCompute(
  1162	  ls: AtomicLogicState,
  1163	  meta: AtomicSelectorMeta,
  1164	  state: any,
  1165	  props: any,
  1166	  tracker: Tracker,
  1167	): any {
  1168	  const inputFns = meta.inputFns || []
  1169	  const inputValues: any[] = []
  1170	  const inputBases: (AtomicDependency | null)[] = []
  1171	
  1172	  for (const fn of inputFns) {
  1173	    const input = getInputValue(ls, fn, state, props)
  1174	    inputValues.push(input.value)
  1175	    inputBases.push(input.baseDep)
  1176	    if (input.baseDep && !isTrackableObject(unwrapProxy(input.value))) {
  1177	      tracker.addDep({ ...input.baseDep, explicit: true })
  1178	    }
  1179	  }
  1180	
  1181	  const result = meta.computeFn ? meta.computeFn(...inputValues) : undefined
  1182	
  1183	  // If the result is exactly one of the input values (identity selector),
  1184	  // record the base dependency so the whole object is tracked.
  1185	  for (let i = 0; i < inputValues.length; i++) {
  1186	    if (inputBases[i]) {
  1187	      const unwrappedInput = unwrapProxy(inputValues[i])
  1188	      if (result === inputValues[i] || (isTrackableObject(result) && unwrapProxy(result) === unwrappedInput)) {
  1189	        tracker.addDep({ ...(inputBases[i] as AtomicDependency), explicit: true })
  1190	      }
  1191	    }
  1192	  }
  1193	
  1194	  // If any input proxy is embedded inside the result (e.g. `{ user }`), we
  1195	  // still depend on the whole input value.
  1196	  if (isTrackableObject(result)) {
  1197	    const visited = new Set<any>()
  1198	    let stack: any[] = [result]
  1199	    for (let depth = 0; stack.length > 0 && depth < 6; depth++) {
  1200	      const next: any[] = []
  1201	      for (const item of stack) {
  1202	        if (visited.has(item)) continue
  1203	        visited.add(item)
  1204	        const raw = unwrapProxy(item)
  1205	        if (!isTrackableObject(raw)) continue
  1206	        for (let i = 0; i < inputValues.length; i++) {
  1207	          if (inputBases[i] && (item === inputValues[i] || raw === unwrapProxy(inputValues[i]))) {
  1208	            tracker.addDep({ ...(inputBases[i] as AtomicDependency), explicit: true })
  1209	          }
  1210	        }
  1211	        if (Array.isArray(raw)) {
  1212	          for (const v of raw) if (isTrackableObject(v)) next.push(v)
  1213	        } else if (raw instanceof Map) {
  1214	          for (const v of raw.values()) if (isTrackableObject(v)) next.push(v)
  1215	        } else if (raw instanceof Set) {
  1216	          for (const v of raw.values()) if (isTrackableObject(v)) next.push(v)
  1217	        } else {
  1218	          for (const key of Object.keys(raw)) {
  1219	            const v = raw[key]
  1220	            if (isTrackableObject(v)) next.push(v)
  1221	          }
  1222	        }
  1223	      }
  1224	      stack = next
  1225	    }
  1226	  }
  1227	
  1228	  return result
  1229	}
  1230	
  1231	function getInputValue(ls: AtomicLogicState, fn: AtomicSelectorFn, state: any, props: any): InputValue {
  1232	  const m = fn._keaAtomicMeta
  1233	
  1234	  if (m && m.props) {
  1235	    const raw = props == null ? undefined : props[m.name]
  1236	    const segments: DepSegment[] = [{ kind: 'prop', key: m.name }]
  1237	    const value = createPropsProxy(raw, segments, ls)
  1238	    return { value, baseDep: { id: `props:${segmentsToId(segments)}`, type: 'props', segments, value: unwrapProxy(value) } }
  1239	  }
  1240	
  1241	  if (m && m.logic === ls.logic) {
  1242	    const value = getLocalAtomicValue(ls, m.name, state, props)
  1243	    if (m.rootDerived) {
  1244	      const segments: DepSegment[] = [{ kind: 'prop', key: m.name }]
  1245	      return { value, baseDep: { id: segmentsToId(segments), type: 'leaf', segments, value: unwrapProxy(value) } }
  1246	    }
  1247	    return { value, baseDep: { id: `selector:${m.name}`, type: 'selector', selectorName: m.name, value: unwrapProxy(value) } }
  1248	  }
  1249	
  1250	  // foreign / plain selector
  1251	  const rawValue = unwrapProxy(fn(state, props))
  1252	  const localName = findLocalSelectorName(ls, fn)
  1253	  const name = localName || 'external'
  1254	  const value = createForeignProxy(rawValue, name, ls)
  1255	  const dep: AtomicDependency = {
  1256	    id: `selector:${name}`,
  1257	    type: 'selector',
  1258	    selectorName: name,
  1259	    foreignFn: fn,
  1260	    value: rawValue,
